*&      Form  get_address_version
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      <--P_ADDR_VERSION  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM GET_ADDRESS_VERSION CHANGING P_ADDR_VERSION.

  SELECT SINGLE NATION FROM TSADVC INTO P_ADDR_VERSION
                 WHERE ACTIVE = 'X'.

  IF SY-SUBRC <> 0.
    P_ADDR_VERSION = ' '.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM. " get_address_version

*&      Form  get_company_adrc_name1
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_WPBP_BUKRS  text
*      -->P_ADDR_VERSION  text
*      <--P_COMPANY_COMNM  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM GET_COMPANY_ADRC_NAME1 USING P_WPBP_BUKRS
                                     P_ADDR_VERSION
                            CHANGING P_COMPANY_COMNM.

  STATICS: ADDR1_SEL LIKE ADDR1_SEL,
           ADDRESS   LIKE ADDR1_VAL.

  SELECT SINGLE ADRNR FROM T001 INTO ADDR1_SEL-ADDRNUMBER
         WHERE  BUKRS  = P_WPBP_BUKRS.

  CHECK ADDR1_SEL-ADDRNUMBER NE SPACE.
  MOVE P_ADDR_VERSION TO ADDR1_SEL-NATION.

  CALL FUNCTION 'ADDR_GET'
    EXPORTING
      ADDRESS_SELECTION = ADDR1_SEL
    IMPORTING
      ADDRESS_VALUE     = ADDRESS
    EXCEPTIONS
      PARAMETER_ERROR   = 1
      ADDRESS_NOT_EXIST = 2
      VERSION_NOT_EXIST = 3
      INTERNAL_ERROR    = 4
      OTHERS            = 5.

  IF SY-SUBRC EQ 0.
    WRITE: ADDRESS-NAME1(25) TO P_COMPANY_COMNM.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM. " get_company_adrc_name1

FORM FILL_EMPLOYEE_INFO.

  ENDIF.

  ENDIF.

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  RE005U
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM RE005U USING $LANGU
                     $LAND1
                     $STATE.

  CHECK T005U-SPRAS NE $LANGU OR T005U-LAND1 NE $LAND1
     OR T005U-BLAND NE $STATE.

  CLEAR T005U.
  SELECT * FROM T005U     WHERE SPRAS = $LANGU
                            AND LAND1 = $LAND1
                            AND BLAND = $STATE.
  ENDSELECT.
  IF SY-SUBRC NE 0.
    CLEAR T005U.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM. " RE005U
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  RE005T
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM RE005T USING $LANGU
                     $NATIO.

  CHECK T005T-SPRAS NE $LANGU OR T005T-LAND1 NE $NATIO.

  CLEAR T005T.
  SELECT * FROM T005T  WHERE SPRAS EQ $LANGU
                         AND LAND1 EQ $NATIO.
  ENDSELECT.

  IF SY-SUBRC NE 0.
    CLEAR T005T.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM. " REOO5T

* QU796286 please refer to internal message 237818 2005
* itab_c-det_itax:      /R81  pkr99_wtr_det_inc_tax
* itab_c-lsp_det_itax   /R84  pkr99_wtr_det_inc_tax_lsp
* itab_c-hon_det_itax   /R85  pkr99_wtr_det_inc_tax_hon
*<<< N1559867 use 'HON_FORM'

*     object  = 'X'   AND  HON_FORM = 'X'.       "HON form
  IF HON_FORM = 'X'.
*>>> N1559867
    DATA  R81    LIKE  RT-BETRG.
    DATA  R84    LIKE  RT-BETRG.
    DATA  R85    LIKE  RT-BETRG.
    DATA  R8485  LIKE  RT-BETRG.
    DATA  GAP    LIKE  RT-BETRG.

    PERFORM READ_RT_BETRG USING PKR99_WTR_DET_INC_TAX     CHANGING R81.

    R8485 = R84 + R85.
    IF R81 <> R8485.
*     Adjust the gap and add to /R84, /R85
      PERFORM TRUNC USING 1 CHANGING  R84.
      PERFORM TRUNC USING 1 CHANGING  R85.

      GAP = R81 - ( R84 + R85 ).
      R84 = R84 + GAP.
*     Re-write the value of /R84, /R85

      CHANGE_CURR R85 ITAB_C-HON_DET_ITAX.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

*<<< N1831602 Terry Begin

      IF ITAB_C-PRE_ENTDT <= '20121231'.

      ELSE.

      ENDIF.
      IF ITAB_C-PRE_ENTDT EQ ENTRY_DAY.
        ITAB_C-CUR_ENTDT = ITAB_C-PRE_RSGNDT + 1.
      ELSE.

      ENDIF.
      MOVE ITAB_C-PRE_ENTDT TO ITAB_C-CALC_ENTDT.
    ELSE.
      IF ITAB_C-CUR_ENTDT <= '20121231'.
        MOVE ITAB_C-CUR_ENTDT  TO ITAB_C-ENTDT_OLD.
      ELSE.
        MOVE ITAB_C-CUR_ENTDT  TO ITAB_C-ENTDT_NEW.
      ENDIF.
      MOVE ITAB_C-CUR_ENTDT TO ITAB_C-CALC_ENTDT.
    ENDIF.

    MOVE ENTRY_DAY  TO ITAB_C-FINAL_ENTDT.

                                   ITAB_C-CUR_RSGNDT
                             CHANGING SVR_PRD.
    MOVE SVR_PRD  TO ITAB_C-CALC_PERIOD.
    CONDENSE ITAB_C-CALC_PERIOD NO-GAPS.

    MOVE ITAB_C-CUR_RSGNDT TO ITAB_C-RSGNDT_NEW.
    IF NOT ITAB_C-ENTDT_OLD IS INITIAL.
*<<< N1848107, N1848108 Terry Begin
      IF ITAB_C-CUR_RSGNDT >= '20121231'.
        MOVE '20121231' TO ITAB_C-RSGNDT_OLD.
      ELSE.
        MOVE ITAB_C-CUR_RSGNDT TO ITAB_C-RSGNDT_OLD.
      ENDIF.
*>>> N1848107, N1848108 Terry End
      IF ITAB_C-RSGNDT_NEW >= '20130101'.
        MOVE '20130101' TO ITAB_C-ENTDT_NEW.
      ENDIF.
      PERFORM CALC_SERVICE_PRD USING ITAB_C-ENTDT_OLD
                                     ITAB_C-RSGNDT_OLD
                               CHANGING SVR_PRD.
      MOVE SVR_PRD  TO ITAB_C-SVR_MONTH_OLD.
      CONDENSE ITAB_C-SVR_MONTH_OLD NO-GAPS.
    ENDIF.

    IF KRSP1-EXMLS <> 0.
      MOVE KRSP1-EXMLS TO ITAB_C-CUR_EXMON.
    ENDIF.

    IF KRSP1-EXMPC <> 0.
      MOVE KRSP1-EXMPC TO ITAB_C-PRE_EXMON.
    ENDIF.

    IF NOT ITAB_C-CUR_EXMON IS INITIAL OR NOT ITAB_C-PRE_EXMON IS INITIAL.
      ITAB_C-CALC_EXC_MONTH = ITAB_C-CUR_EXMON + ITAB_C-PRE_EXMON.
      CONDENSE ITAB_C-CALC_EXC_MONTH NO-GAPS.
    ENDIF.

    IF KRSP1-EXMPC <> 0 OR KRSP1-EXMLS <> 0.
      IF SVR_PRD <> 0.
        ITAB_C-EXC_MONTH_OLD = ITAB_C-CUR_EXMON + ITAB_C-PRE_EXMON.

      ELSE.
        ITAB_C-EXC_MONTH_NEW = ITAB_C-CUR_EXMON + ITAB_C-PRE_EXMON.
        CONDENSE ITAB_C-EXC_MONTH_NEW NO-GAPS.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    CONDENSE ITAB_C-PRE_SVR NO-GAPS.
    IF KRSP1-SVRPR <> 0.
      ITAB_C-PRE_PERIOD = CEIL( ( ITAB_C-PRE_SVR
                        - ITAB_C-PRE_EXMON + ITAB_C-ADD_MON ) / 12 ).
      CONDENSE ITAB_C-PRE_PERIOD NO-GAPS.

    PERFORM CALC_SERVICE_PRD USING ITAB_C-CUR_ENTDT
                                   ITAB_C-CUR_RSGNDT
                             CHANGING SVR_PRD.

    IF SVR_PRD <> 0.
      MOVE SVR_PRD     TO ITAB_C-SVR_MONTH.
      CONDENSE ITAB_C-SVR_MONTH NO-GAPS.
      ITAB_C-CUR_PERIOD = CEIL( ( ITAB_C-SVR_MONTH
                        - ITAB_C-CUR_EXMON ) / 12 ).

    ENDIF.

                                   ITAB_C-RSGNDT_NEW
                             CHANGING SVR_PRD.
    MOVE SVR_PRD  TO ITAB_C-SVR_MONTH_NEW.

    CONDENSE ITAB_C-SVR_MONTH_NEW NO-GAPS.

    IF NOT ITAB_C-SVR_MONTH_OLD IS INITIAL.

    ENDIF.
    CONDENSE ITAB_C-SVR_PERIOD_NEW NO-GAPS.
    CONDENSE ITAB_C-SVR_PERIOD_OLD NO-GAPS.

    L_ORI_TOT_TAXB_YEAR = TRUNC( TOT_TAXBASE / ITAB_C-TAX_SPERIOD
                        * 100 ) / 100.

    PERFORM READ_RT_BETRG USING PKR99_WTR_AVE_TAXABLE_Y_HON CHANGING AMOUNT.
    L_ORI_HON_TAXB_YEAR = TRUNC( AMOUNT / 5 * 100 ) / 100.
    PERFORM READ_RT_BETRG USING PKR99_WTR_AVE_INC_TAX_Y_HON CHANGING AMOUNT.
    L_ORI_HON_TAX_YEAR = TRUNC( AMOUNT / 5 * 100 ) / 100.
    PERFORM READ_RT_BETRG USING PKR99_WTR_AVE_INC_TAX_Y CHANGING AMOUNT.
    L_ORI_TOT_TAX_YEAR = AMOUNT + L_ORI_HON_TAX_YEAR.
    PERFORM READ_RT_BETRG USING PKR99_WTR_CAL_DEFER_RES_TAX
                          CHANGING  L_DEFER_RTAX.
    1_DEFER_TOTAL = DEFER_TAX + L_DEFER_RTAX.
    CHANGE_CURR:  L_ORI_HON_TAXB_YEAR ITAB_C-ORI_HON_TAXB_YEAR,
                  L_ORI_HON_TAX_YEAR  ITAB_C-ORI_HON_TAX_YEAR,
                  L_ORI_TOT_TAXB_YEAR ITAB_C-ORI_TOT_TAXB_YEAR,
                  L_ORI_TOT_TAX_YEAR  ITAB_C-ORI_TOT_TAX_YEAR,
                  L_DEFER_RTAX        ITAB_C-DEFER_RTAX,
                  1_DEFER_TOTAL       ITAB_C-DEFER_TOTAL.

    L_DEPTT = ITAB_C-DEPTT.
    L_TAXBASE = ITAB_C-TAXBASE.
    L_DET_STAX = ITAB_C-DET_STAX.
    L_SPY01 = ITAB_C-SPY01.
    CONDENSE L_DEPTT NO-GAPS.
    CONDENSE L_TAXBASE NO-GAPS.
    CONDENSE L_DET_STAX NO-GAPS.
    CONDENSE L_SPY01 NO-GAPS.
    IF L_DEPTT NE '0'.
      ITAB_C-DET_TAX_DEFER = ITAB_C-DET_ITAX.
      ITAB_C-SEP_PAY_DEFER = ITAB_C-SPY00.
      ITAB_C-DEFER_TAX_DEFER = ITAB_C-DEFER_TAX.
    ELSE.
      CLEAR ITAB_C-DET_TAX_DEFER.
      CLEAR ITAB_C-SEP_PAY_DEFER.
      CLEAR ITAB_C-DEFER_TAX_DEFER.
      CLEAR ITAB_C-DEPTT.
    ENDIF.
    IF L_TAXBASE NE '0'.
      MOVE ITAB_C-PRE_ITAX TO ITAB_C-PRE_PAID_OLD.
      MOVE '0' TO ITAB_C-PRE_PAID_NEW.
    ELSE.
      MOVE ITAB_C-PRE_ITAX TO ITAB_C-PRE_PAID_NEW.
      MOVE '0' TO ITAB_C-PRE_PAID_OLD.
    ENDIF.
    IF L_DET_STAX EQ '0'.
      CLEAR ITAB_C-DET_STAX.
    ENDIF.
    IF L_SPY01 EQ '0'.
      CLEAR ITAB_C-SPY01.
    ENDIF.
*<<< N1848107, N1848108 Terry Begin
    IF ITAB_C-ENTDT_NEW IS INITIAL.
      CLEAR ITAB_C-ENTDT_NEW.
      CLEAR ITAB_C-RSGNDT_NEW.
      CLEAR ITAB_C-SVR_MONTH_NEW.
      CLEAR ITAB_C-EXC_MONTH_NEW.
      CLEAR ITAB_C-ADD_MONTH_NEW.
      CLEAR ITAB_C-SVR_PERIOD_NEW.
    ENDIF.
*>>> N1848107, N1848108 Terry End
  ENDIF.
*>>> N1831602 Terry End
  APPEND ITAB_C.
ENDFORM. " FILL_ITAB_C
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  CALC_SERVICE_PRD
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_ENTDT  text
*      -->P_RSGNDT  text
*      <--P_SVR_PRD  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM CALC_SERVICE_PRD USING P_ENTDT
                               P_RSGNDT
                      CHANGING P_SVR_PRD.
  DATA: P_DAYS      TYPE P,
        P_MONTHS    TYPE P,
        P_YEARS     TYPE P.

* CALL FUNCTION 'HRVE_GET_TIME_BETWEEN_DATES'          "CLC Note1119154
*<<< N1841568 Terry Begin
*  CALL FUNCTION 'HR_GET_TIME_BETWEEN_DATES'          "CLC Note1119154
  CALL FUNCTION 'HR_KR_GET_TIME_BETWEEN_DATES'
*>>> N1841568 Terry End
    EXPORTING
      BEG_DATE = P_ENTDT
      END_DATE = P_RSGNDT
    IMPORTING
      DAYS     = P_DAYS
      MONTHS   = P_MONTHS
      YEARS    = P_YEARS.

  IF P_DAYS >= 1.
    P_MONTHS = P_MONTHS + 1.
  ENDIF.

  IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
    P_SVR_PRD = P_YEARS * 12 + P_MONTHS.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM. " CALC_SERVICE_PRD

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  RE7KRP1
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM RE7KRP1 USING P_BIZNO.
  CHECK P_BIZNO NE T7KRP1-BIZNO.
  SELECT        * FROM  T7KRP1
         WHERE  SPRSL  = SY-LANGU
         AND    BIZNO  = P_BIZNO.
    EXIT.
  ENDSELECT.
  IF SY-SUBRC NE 0.
    CLEAR T7KRP1.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM. " RE7KRP1
*---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       READ_T596F                                                    *
*---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Lesen des zeitabh?gigen Anteils des Formularnamens           *
*---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  $sname                                                        *
*       $datum                                                        *
*  <--  $forml                                                        *
*---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM READ_T596F USING VALUE($SNAME) "neu "YMTK020198
                      VALUE($DATUM) LIKE SY-DATUM
                CHANGING VALUE($FORML).
  IF T596F-SNAME NE $SNAME OR
     T596F-BEGDA GT $DATUM OR
     T596F-ENDDA LT $DATUM.
    SELECT * FROM T596F WHERE SNAME EQ $SNAME
                        AND   BEGDA LE $DATUM
                        AND   ENDDA GE $DATUM.
    ENDSELECT.
    IF SY-SUBRC NE 0.
      CLEAR T596F.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
  $FORML = T596F-MODNA.

ENDFORM. "READ_T596F
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  READ_RT_BETRG
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM READ_RT_BETRG USING P_LGART
                   CHANGING AMT.

  READ TABLE RT WITH KEY LGART = P_LGART.
  IF SY-SUBRC EQ 0.
    AMT = RT-BETRG.
  ELSE.
    AMT = 0.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM. " READ_RT_BETRG
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  ERROR_HANDLING
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*

FORM ERROR_HANDLING USING PERNR MSGNO MSGV1 MSGV2 MSGV3 MSGV4.
  CLEAR ERROR_TAB.
  ERROR_TAB-PERNR = PERNR.
  ERROR_TAB-ARBGB = 'HRPAYKR01'.
  ERROR_TAB-MSGTY = 'E'.
  ERROR_TAB-MSGNO = MSGNO.
  ERROR_TAB-MSGV1 = MSGV1.
  ERROR_TAB-MSGV2 = MSGV2.
  ERROR_TAB-MSGV3 = MSGV3.
  ERROR_TAB-MSGV4 = MSGV4.
  APPEND ERROR_TAB.

*************Start of Coding by c5056154 on 02.12.04*******************
  DATA: LV_MSGV1 TYPE SY-MSGV1,
        LV_MSGV2 TYPE SY-MSGV2,
        LV_MSGV3 TYPE SY-MSGV3,
        LV_MSGV4 TYPE SY-MSGV4.

  MOVE: MSGV1 TO LV_MSGV1,
        MSGV2 TO LV_MSGV2,
        MSGV3 TO LV_MSGV3,
        MSGV4 TO LV_MSGV4.

  IF NOT PERNR IS INITIAL.
    MESSAGE ID 'HRPAYKR01' TYPE OBJ_PAL_LOG->C_ERROR
      NUMBER MSGNO
      WITH LV_MSGV1 LV_MSGV2 LV_MSGV3 LV_MSGV4
      INTO OBJ_PAL_LOG->G_MSG_TEXT.
    OBJ_PAL_LOG->CREATE_PERNR_MSG(                        "ACC
      P_PERNR = PERNR ).
*    CALL FUNCTION 'HR_PAL_PERNR_MSG_ADD'
*      EXPORTING
*        i_pernr = pernr
*        i_msgid = 'HRPAYKR01'

FORM READ_RGDIR.
  DATA: TEM_RGDIR   LIKE RGDIR OCCURS 0.
  DATA: BEGIN_DATE  TYPE D,
        END_DATE    TYPE D.

*start of HUANGHA1448559
  DATA: ADJ_START_DT   TYPE D,
        ADJ_END_DT     TYPE D.
*end of HUANGHA1448559

  IF END_DT IS INITIAL.
    END_DT = START_DT.
  ENDIF.

  CALL FUNCTION 'CU_READ_RGDIR'
    EXPORTING
      PERSNR          = PERNR-PERNR
    TABLES
      IN_RGDIR        = TEM_RGDIR
    EXCEPTIONS
      NO_RECORD_FOUND = 1
      OTHERS          = 2.

  IF SY-SUBRC NE 0.
    PERFORM ERROR_HANDLING USING PERNR-PERNR '405'
                                 SPACE SPACE SPACE SPACE.

    REJECT.
  ENDIF.

*start of HUANGHA1448559
*check separation date or agreement date falls in selection period
*and it determines run period for separation or mid-separation

  ADJ_START_DT = START_DT.
  ADJ_END_DT = END_DT.

  IF OBJECT <> 'X'.
    LOOP AT P0538.
      IF ( P0538-AGPDT GE START_DT AND P0538-AGPDT LE END_DT )
*<<< N1561401 Tax Deferring
      OR ( P0538-OSPDT GE START_DT AND P0538-OSPDT LE END_DT AND
           P0538-ADJST EQ '1' )
*>>> N1561401
      OR ( P0538-SEPDT GE START_DT AND P0538-SEPDT LE END_DT AND
           P0538-AGPDT IS INITIAL ).
* Adjust run period to get RT of satisfied separation date
        IF P0538-BEGDA < START_DT.
          ADJ_START_DT = P0538-BEGDA.
        ELSEIF P0538-BEGDA > END_DT.
          ADJ_END_DT = P0538-BEGDA.
        ENDIF.
      ELSE.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDIF.
*end of HUANGHA1448559


  CALL FUNCTION 'CD_READ_DATE_RANGE_ACTUAL_NV'
    EXPORTING
      BEGDA     = ADJ_START_DT                          "HUANGHA1448559
      ENDDA     = ADJ_END_DT                            "HUANGHA1448559
    TABLES
      RGDIR_IN  = TEM_RGDIR
      RGDIR_OUT = RGDIR.

ENDFORM. "READ_RGDIR

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  FILL_B_RECORD
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM FILL_B_RECORD. "NAN 200108
  DATA:    NUM(12)        TYPE N.
  STATICS  SEQ            TYPE I.
  DATA : N_INCOME(13)     TYPE N,
         N_TOT_INCOME(13) TYPE N.
  CLEAR: N_INCOME,  N_TOT_INCOME.
  CLEAR B_RECORD.

  READ TABLE BUPLA_DETAIL_TAB WITH KEY PERNR-PERNR.

  MOVE  BUPLA_DETAIL_TAB-BUKRS   TO B_RECORD-BUKRS.

  MOVE: 'B'                      TO B_RECORD-R_MODIF,
        '22'                     TO B_RECORD-D_MODIF.
* Tax office
  MOVE: C_RECORD-TAX_OFF         TO B_RECORD-TAX_OFF.
* Sequence number
  SORT B_RECORD BY TAX_OFF STCD2 SEQNO DESCENDING.
  LOOP AT B_RECORD WHERE  TAX_OFF =  BUPLA_DETAIL_TAB-TAXOFF
                     AND  STCD2   =  BUPLA_DETAIL_TAB-STCD2.
    EXIT.
  ENDLOOP.
  IF SY-SUBRC EQ 0.
    B_RECORD-SEQNO = 0.
  ELSE.
*    B_RECORD-SEQNO = 1.
    SEQ = SEQ + 1.
    B_RECORD-SEQNO = SEQ.
  ENDIF.
* Business number, company name, president,resident number
  MOVE: BUPLA_DETAIL_TAB-STCD2         TO B_RECORD-STCD2,
        BUPLA_DETAIL_TAB-NAME          TO B_RECORD-COMNM,
        BUPLA_DETAIL_TAB-KR_REPRES     TO B_RECORD-REPRES,
        BUPLA_DETAIL_TAB-STCD1         TO B_RECORD-STCD1.
* Num of c records
  MOVE 0               TO B_RECORD-C_REC_NUM.
  LOOP AT C_RECORD WHERE PERNR = PERNR-PERNR.
    B_RECORD-C_REC_NUM = B_RECORD-C_REC_NUM + 1.
*   MOVE D_RECORD-TOT02 TO N_INCOME.
    MOVE C_RECORD-TOT00 TO N_INCOME.   "Jaekie
    ADD  N_INCOME       TO N_TOT_INCOME.
  ENDLOOP.
* Num of d records
  MOVE 0               TO B_RECORD-D_REC_NUM.
  LOOP AT D_RECORD WHERE PERNR = PERNR-PERNR.
    B_RECORD-D_REC_NUM = B_RECORD-D_REC_NUM + 1.
    MOVE D_RECORD-TOT02 TO N_INCOME.
    ADD  N_INCOME       TO N_TOT_INCOME.
  ENDLOOP.
* ----------------------------------------------------------Jaekie
* MOVE C_RECORD-TOT00     TO N_INCOME.
* ADD  N_INCOME           TO N_TOT_INCOME.
* ----------------------------------------------------------Jaekie
  MOVE N_TOT_INCOME       TO B_RECORD-TOT_SEPAR.
* Total determined income tax
  MOVE_CNP  C_RECORD-DET_ITAX    B_RECORD-INC_TAX.
* Legal tax   N/A fill with 0
  MOVE  0    TO  B_RECORD-LEGAL_TAX.
* Special tax, resident tax, total tax.
  MOVE_CNP  C_RECORD-DET_STAX    B_RECORD-DET_STAX.
  MOVE_CNP  C_RECORD-DET_RTAX    B_RECORD-DET_RTAX.
  MOVE_CNP  C_RECORD-DET_TOTAL1  B_RECORD-DET_TOTAL.
  MOVE     SPACE       TO  B_RECORD-CORRECTION.
  MOVE     SPACE       TO  B_RECORD-BSPACE.

*<<< N1432309: submit period
  IF P_ANNU = 'X'.
    B_RECORD-SUBMIT_PERIOD = '1'.
  ELSEIF P_IRCL = 'X'.
    B_RECORD-SUBMIT_PERIOD = '2'.
  ELSE.
    B_RECORD-SUBMIT_PERIOD = '3'.
  ENDIF.
*>>> N1432309

  COLLECT B_RECORD.
ENDFORM. " FILL_B_RECORD

FORM FILL_A_RECORD. "NAN 200108
  DATA:  NUM_B    TYPE  I,

         NUM_D    TYPE  I,                                  "N1559867
         PAYDATE  TYPE  D.

    MOVE NUM_B TO A_RECORD-B_REC_NUM_11.                    "N1682182

        NUM_B                     TO A_RECORD-B_REC_NUM_11, "N1682182
        '101'                     TO A_RECORD-HANGLE_CODE.
*<<< N1559867 Submitted Date

    MOVE SUB_DATE TO A_RECORD-SUBMITDT.
  ENDIF.
*>>> N1559867
* Period class

  MOVE  KRSP1-SEPDT TO PAYDATE.
  IF  PAYDATE+4(2) GE 01 AND PAYDATE+4(2) LE 06.
    MOVE '1' TO  A_RECORD-TARGET_PRD.
  ELSEIF PAYDATE+4(2) GE 07 AND PAYDATE+4(2) LE 12.
    MOVE '2' TO  A_RECORD-TARGET_PRD.
  ELSE.

  ENDIF.
* QU796286
  MOVE  TAX_ID    TO  A_RECORD-HOME_TAX_ID.
  MOVE  SUB_DEPT  TO  A_RECORD-SUB_DEPT.
  MOVE  SUB_NAME  TO  A_RECORD-SUB_NAME.

         SPACE  TO A_RECORD-ASPACE.

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  WRITE_TO_TEMSE
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE. "NAN 200108
  CHECK TEMSE_OPEN = TRUE.
  PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD USING 'A'.

  SORT B_RECORD BY BUKRS SEQNO.
  SORT C_RECORD BY BUKRS TAX_OFF SEQNO.
  SORT D_RECORD BY BUKRS TAX_OFF SEQNO D_SEQNO.

  LOOP AT B_RECORD WHERE BUKRS = A_RECORD-BUKRS.
    PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD USING 'B'.
    LOOP AT C_RECORD WHERE TAX_OFF = B_RECORD-TAX_OFF
                       AND STCD2   = B_RECORD-STCD2
                       AND BUKRS   = B_RECORD-BUKRS.
      PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD USING 'C'.
      LOOP AT D_RECORD  WHERE PERNR = C_RECORD-PERNR.
        PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD USING 'D'.
      ENDLOOP.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDLOOP.
ENDFORM. " WRITE_TO_TEMSE
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  WRITE_TO_TEMSE_2001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_2001. "NAN507759
  CHECK TEMSE_OPEN = TRUE.
  PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_01 USING 'A'.

  SORT B_RECORD BY BUKRS SEQNO.
  SORT C_RECORD BY BUKRS TAX_OFF SEQNO.
  SORT D_RECORD BY BUKRS TAX_OFF SEQNO D_SEQNO.

  LOOP AT B_RECORD WHERE BUKRS = A_RECORD-BUKRS.
    PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_01 USING 'B'.
    LOOP AT C_RECORD WHERE TAX_OFF = B_RECORD-TAX_OFF
                       AND STCD2   = B_RECORD-STCD2
                       AND BUKRS   = B_RECORD-BUKRS.
      PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_01 USING 'C'.
      LOOP AT D_RECORD  WHERE PERNR = C_RECORD-PERNR.
        PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_01 USING 'D'.
      ENDLOOP.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDLOOP.
ENDFORM. " WRITE_TO_TEMSE_2001
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  WRITE_TO_TEMSE_2002
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_2002. "NAN507759

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD USING RECORDNAME. "NAN 200108
  CLEAR DATAFILE. REFRESH DATAFILE.

  CASE RECORDNAME.
    WHEN 'A'.
      PERFORM  ADD_ARECORD_TO_DATAFILE.
    WHEN 'B'.
      PERFORM  ADD_BRECORD_TO_DATAFILE.
    WHEN 'C'.
      PERFORM  ADD_CRECORD_TO_DATAFILE.
    WHEN 'D'.
      PERFORM  ADD_DRECORD_TO_DATAFILE.
  ENDCASE.
*  APPEND datafile.    NAN628548
  CALL FUNCTION 'RSTS_WRITE'
    EXPORTING
      FBHANDLE = OBJHD
    TABLES
      DATATAB  = DATAFILE.
ENDFORM. " WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD

*              ADD_ARECORD_TO_DATAFILE                                 *
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM ADD_ARECORD_TO_DATAFILE. "NAN 200108
  MOVE :   A_RECORD-R_MODIF        TO DATAFILE+0(1),
           A_RECORD-D_MODIF        TO DATAFILE+1(2),
           A_RECORD-TAX_OFF        TO DATAFILE+3(3),
           A_RECORD-SUBMITDT       TO DATAFILE+6(8),
           A_RECORD-AGENCY         TO DATAFILE+14(1),
           A_RECORD-AGENT_NUM      TO DATAFILE+15(6),
           A_RECORD-STCD2          TO DATAFILE+21(10),
           A_RECORD-COMNM          TO DATAFILE+31(40),
           A_RECORD-STCD1          TO DATAFILE+71(13),
           A_RECORD-REPRES         TO DATAFILE+84(30),
           A_RECORD-AG_TELNR       TO DATAFILE+114(15),
* UNPACK   A_RECORD-B_REC_NUM      TO DATAFILE+129(5).
           A_RECORD-B_REC_NUM      TO DATAFILE+129(5).

  MOVE :   A_RECORD-HANGLE_CODE    TO DATAFILE+134(3),
           A_RECORD-TARGET_PRD     TO DATAFILE+137(1),
           A_RECORD-CORRECTION     TO DATAFILE+138(1),
           A_RECORD-ASPACE         TO DATAFILE+139(191).
* Unicode NAN628548
  IF CL_ABAP_CHAR_UTILITIES=>CHARSIZE > 1.

    CLEAR  OUT_XSTRING.  CLEAR  ADJ_STRING. CLEAR  ADJ_XSTRING.
* Convert unicode string to hex stream
    PERFORM UNISTRING_TO_XSTRING USING   DATAFILE+0(71)  71.
    MOVE OUT_XSTRING TO  ADJ_XSTRING.

    PERFORM UNISTRING_TO_XSTRING USING   DATAFILE+71(43) 43.
    CONCATENATE ADJ_XSTRING OUT_XSTRING INTO ADJ_XSTRING IN BYTE MODE.

    PERFORM UNISTRING_TO_XSTRING USING   DATAFILE+114(116) 116.
    CONCATENATE ADJ_XSTRING OUT_XSTRING INTO ADJ_XSTRING IN BYTE MODE.

* Comvert hex stream to unicode string
    PERFORM  XSTRING_TO_UNISTRING.
    CLEAR DATAFILE. REFRESH DATAFILE.
    APPEND ADJ_STRING TO DATAFILE.
* Unicode end
  ELSE.
    APPEND DATAFILE.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM. "ADD_ARECORD_TO_DATAFILE

*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*              ADD_BRECORD_TO_DATAFILE                                 *
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM ADD_BRECORD_TO_DATAFILE. "NAN 200108

  MOVE :  B_RECORD-R_MODIF         TO  DATAFILE+0(1),
          B_RECORD-D_MODIF         TO  DATAFILE+1(2),
          B_RECORD-TAX_OFF         TO  DATAFILE+3(3).
  UNPACK  B_RECORD-SEQNO           TO  DATAFILE+6(6).
  MOVE :  B_RECORD-STCD2           TO  DATAFILE+12(10),
          B_RECORD-COMNM           TO  DATAFILE+22(40),
          B_RECORD-REPRES          TO  DATAFILE+62(30),
          B_RECORD-STCD1           TO  DATAFILE+92(13).
  UNPACK:
          B_RECORD-C_REC_NUM       TO  DATAFILE+105(7),
          B_RECORD-D_REC_NUM       TO  DATAFILE+112(7),
          B_RECORD-TOT_SEPAR       TO  DATAFILE+119(14),
          B_RECORD-INC_TAX         TO  DATAFILE+133(13),
          B_RECORD-LEGAL_TAX       TO  DATAFILE+146(13),
          B_RECORD-DET_STAX        TO  DATAFILE+159(13),
          B_RECORD-DET_RTAX        TO  DATAFILE+172(13),
          B_RECORD-DET_TOTAL       TO  DATAFILE+185(13).
  MOVE:
          B_RECORD-CORRECTION      TO  DATAFILE+198(1),
          B_RECORD-BSPACE          TO  DATAFILE+199(131).
* Unicode NAN628548
  IF CL_ABAP_CHAR_UTILITIES=>CHARSIZE > 1.
    CLEAR  OUT_XSTRING.  CLEAR  ADJ_STRING. CLEAR  ADJ_XSTRING.
* Convert unicode string to hex stream
    PERFORM UNISTRING_TO_XSTRING USING   DATAFILE+0(62)  62.
    MOVE OUT_XSTRING TO  ADJ_XSTRING.

    PERFORM UNISTRING_TO_XSTRING USING   DATAFILE+62(30) 30.
    CONCATENATE ADJ_XSTRING OUT_XSTRING INTO ADJ_XSTRING IN BYTE MODE.

    PERFORM UNISTRING_TO_XSTRING USING   DATAFILE+92(238) 238.
    CONCATENATE ADJ_XSTRING OUT_XSTRING INTO ADJ_XSTRING IN BYTE MODE.
* Comvert hex stream to unicode string
    PERFORM  XSTRING_TO_UNISTRING.
    CLEAR DATAFILE. REFRESH DATAFILE.
    APPEND ADJ_STRING TO DATAFILE.
* Unicode end
  ELSE.
    APPEND DATAFILE.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM. " ADD_BRECORD_TO_DATAFILE

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  unistring_to_xstring
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM UNISTRING_TO_XSTRING USING RECORD TYPE C "NAN628548
                                  LENGTH  TYPE I.

  DATA: IN_STRING TYPE STRING.

  IN_STRING = RECORD.

  CALL FUNCTION 'HR_KR_STRING_TO_XSTRING'
    EXPORTING
      UNICODE_STRING   = IN_STRING
      OUT_LEN          = LENGTH
    IMPORTING
      XSTRING_STREAM   = OUT_XSTRING
    EXCEPTIONS
      INVALID_CODEPAGE = 1
      INVALID_STRING   = 2.

ENDFORM. " unistring_to_xstring
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  xstring_to_unicode
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM XSTRING_TO_UNISTRING . "NAN628548

  CALL FUNCTION 'HR_KR_XSTRING_TO_STRING'
    EXPORTING
      IN_XSTRING = ADJ_XSTRING
*     out_len    = 330
    IMPORTING
      OUT_STRING = ADJ_STRING.

ENDFORM. " xstring_to_unicode

FORM CLOSE_DATASET. "NAN 200108

    EXPORTING
      FBHANDLE       = OBJHD
    EXCEPTIONS
      FB_CALL_HANDLE = 1
      FB_ERROR       = 2
      FB_RSTS_OTHER  = 3
      OTHERS         = 4.

  ENDIF.
ENDFORM. " CLOSE_DATASET

*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*                  SET_STATUS_INIT                                     *
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM SET_STATUS_INIT USING VALUE(P_FCODE1) "NAN 200108
                            VALUE(P_FCODE2) VALUE(P_FCODE3).
  REFRESH STATUS_TAB.
  CLEAR   STATUS_TAB.
  IF NOT P_FCODE1 IS INITIAL.
    APPEND P_FCODE1 TO STATUS_TAB.
  ENDIF.
  IF NOT P_FCODE2 IS INITIAL.
    APPEND P_FCODE2 TO STATUS_TAB.
  ENDIF.
  IF NOT P_FCODE3 IS INITIAL.
    APPEND P_FCODE3 TO STATUS_TAB.
  ENDIF.

* In case there are errors, show
  DESCRIBE TABLE ERROR_TAB LINES NUMBER_OF_ERROR_LINES.
  IF NUMBER_OF_ERROR_LINES EQ 0.
    APPEND 'ERR' TO STATUS_TAB.
  ENDIF.

  SET PF-STATUS 'INIT' EXCLUDING STATUS_TAB.
ENDFORM. " SET_STATUS_INIT

         B_RECORD-INC_TAX TO GS_OUTTAB5-VALUE.

         B_RECORD-DET_TOTAL TO GS_OUTTAB5-VALUE.
  APPEND  GS_OUTTAB5 TO GT_OUTTAB5.

  GV_FLAG = 'Z'.

** Find all RGDIR

*    EXPORTING

*    TABLES
*      IN_RGDIR        = RGDIR_ALL

*  delete table rgdir_all from rgdir.

*  loop at rgdir_all where occat  = pkr99_occ_sep    or
*                          occat  = pkr99_occ_mid_sep.
*  endloop.
*  check sy-subrc = 0.

*BAIW1025771 Begin

    CHECK LINE_NUMBER > 1 .  "means there Mid Sep
  ENDIF.

* end of Note 824400 DP

                        CHANGING HON_AMT.

    P_EE_TYPE = 'HON'.
  ELSE.

                          CHANGING HON_AMT.

*&      Form  FILL_B_RECORD_HON
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM FILL_B_RECORD_HON . "QU796286
  DATA:    NUM(12)        TYPE N.
  STATICS  SEQ            TYPE I.
  DATA : N_INCOME(14)     TYPE N,
         N_TOT_INCOME(14) TYPE N,
         N_DEPTT_SEP(14)  TYPE N,                           "N1658163
         N_TOT_DEP_SEP(14) TYPE N,                          "N1658163
         N_DEPTT_HON(14)  TYPE N,                           "N1658163
         N_TOT_DEP_HON(14) TYPE N.                          "N1658163
  CLEAR: N_INCOME,  N_TOT_INCOME.
  CLEAR B_RECORD.

  READ TABLE BUPLA_DETAIL_TAB WITH KEY PERNR-PERNR.

  MOVE  BUPLA_DETAIL_TAB-BUKRS   TO B_RECORD-BUKRS.

  MOVE: 'B'                      TO B_RECORD-R_MODIF,
        '22'                     TO B_RECORD-D_MODIF.
* Tax office
  MOVE: C_RECORD_HON-TAX_OFF         TO B_RECORD-TAX_OFF.
* Sequence number
  SORT B_RECORD BY TAX_OFF STCD2 SEQNO DESCENDING.
  LOOP AT B_RECORD WHERE  TAX_OFF =  BUPLA_DETAIL_TAB-TAXOFF
                     AND  STCD2   =  BUPLA_DETAIL_TAB-STCD2.
    EXIT.
  ENDLOOP.
  IF SY-SUBRC EQ 0.
    B_RECORD-SEQNO = 0.
  ELSE.
*    B_RECORD-SEQNO = 1.
    SEQ = SEQ + 1.
    B_RECORD-SEQNO = SEQ.
  ENDIF.
* Business number, company name, president,resident number
  MOVE: BUPLA_DETAIL_TAB-STCD2         TO B_RECORD-STCD2,
        BUPLA_DETAIL_TAB-NAME          TO B_RECORD-COMNM,
        BUPLA_DETAIL_TAB-KR_REPRES     TO B_RECORD-REPRES,
        BUPLA_DETAIL_TAB-STCD1         TO B_RECORD-STCD1.
* Num of c records
  MOVE 0               TO B_RECORD-C_REC_NUM.
  LOOP AT C_RECORD_HON WHERE PERNR = PERNR-PERNR.
    B_RECORD-C_REC_NUM = B_RECORD-C_REC_NUM + 1.
*   MOVE D_RECORD-TOT02 TO N_INCOME.
    "<<< N1536031
    IF END_DT(4) LT '2010'.
      MOVE C_RECORD_HON-TOT00 TO N_INCOME.   "Jaekie
    ELSE.
      MOVE C_RECORD_HON-TOT_AMT TO N_INCOME.
      MOVE C_RECORD_HON-DEPTT_SEP TO N_DEPTT_SEP.           "N1658163
      MOVE C_RECORD_HON-DEPTT_HON TO N_DEPTT_HON.           "N1658163
    ENDIF.
    ">>> N1536031
    ADD  N_INCOME       TO N_TOT_INCOME.
    ADD  N_DEPTT_SEP    TO N_TOT_DEP_SEP.                   "N1658163
    ADD  N_DEPTT_HON    TO N_TOT_DEP_HON.                   "N1658163
  ENDLOOP.
* Num of d records
  MOVE 0               TO B_RECORD-D_REC_NUM.
  LOOP AT D_RECORD WHERE PERNR = PERNR-PERNR.
    B_RECORD-D_REC_NUM = B_RECORD-D_REC_NUM + 1.
    "<<< N1536031
    IF END_DT(4) LT '2010'.
      MOVE D_RECORD-TOT02 TO N_INCOME.
      ADD  N_INCOME       TO N_TOT_INCOME.
    ENDIF.
    ">>> N1536031
  ENDLOOP.
* ----------------------------------------------------------Jaekie
* MOVE C_RECORD-TOT00     TO N_INCOME.
* ADD  N_INCOME           TO N_TOT_INCOME.
* ----------------------------------------------------------Jaekie
  MOVE N_TOT_INCOME       TO B_RECORD-TOT_SEPAR.
  MOVE N_TOT_DEP_SEP      TO B_RECORD-TOT_DEP_SEP.          "N1658163
  MOVE N_TOT_DEP_HON      TO B_RECORD-TOT_DEP_HON.          "N1658163
* Total determined income tax
  MOVE_CNP  C_RECORD_HON-DET_ITAX    B_RECORD-INC_TAX.
* Legal tax   N/A fill with 0
  MOVE  0    TO  B_RECORD-LEGAL_TAX.
* Special tax, resident tax, total tax.
  MOVE_CNP  C_RECORD_HON-DET_STAX    B_RECORD-DET_STAX.
  MOVE_CNP  C_RECORD_HON-DET_RTAX    B_RECORD-DET_RTAX.
  MOVE_CNP  C_RECORD_HON-DET_TOTAL1  B_RECORD-DET_TOTAL.
  MOVE     SPACE       TO  B_RECORD-CORRECTION.
  MOVE     SPACE       TO  B_RECORD-BSPACE.
* SHAO796286
  MOVE   SPACE TO B_RECORD-BSPACE_HON.
  MOVE   '25'   TO B_RECORD-D_MODIF_HON.
*<<< N1432309: submit period
  IF P_ANNU = 'X'.
    B_RECORD-SUBMIT_PERIOD = '1'.
  ELSEIF P_IRCL = 'X'.
    B_RECORD-SUBMIT_PERIOD = '2'.
  ELSE.
    B_RECORD-SUBMIT_PERIOD = '3'.
  ENDIF.
*>>> N1432309
  MOVE '1'  TO B_RECORD-TAXPAYER.                           "N1800801
  COLLECT B_RECORD.

ENDFORM. " FILL_B_RECORD_HON
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  write_to_temse_2004
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_FILE_TYPE  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_2004 USING FILE_TYPE. "QU796286
  CHECK TEMSE_OPEN = TRUE.
  IF FILE_TYPE = 'LSP'.
    PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_04 USING 'A' FILE_TYPE.

    SORT B_RECORD BY BUKRS SEQNO.
*    SORT C_RECORD BY BUKRS TAX_OFF SEQNO.          "QU796286
    SORT C_RECORD BY BUKRS TAX_OFF STCD2 SEQNO.             "QU796286
    SORT D_RECORD BY BUKRS TAX_OFF SEQNO D_SEQNO.

    LOOP AT B_RECORD WHERE BUKRS = A_RECORD-BUKRS.
      PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_04 USING 'B' FILE_TYPE.
      LOOP AT C_RECORD WHERE TAX_OFF = B_RECORD-TAX_OFF
                         AND STCD2   = B_RECORD-STCD2
                        AND BUKRS   = B_RECORD-BUKRS.
        PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_04 USING 'C' FILE_TYPE.
        LOOP AT D_RECORD  WHERE PERNR = C_RECORD-PERNR.
          PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_04 USING 'D' FILE_TYPE.
        ENDLOOP.
      ENDLOOP.
    ENDLOOP.
  ELSEIF FILE_TYPE = 'HON'.
    PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_04 USING 'A' FILE_TYPE.

    SORT B_RECORD BY BUKRS SEQNO.
*    SORT C_RECORD_HON BY BUKRS TAX_OFF SEQNO.      "QU796286
    SORT C_RECORD_HON BY BUKRS TAX_OFF STCD2 SEQNO.         "QU796286
    SORT D_RECORD BY BUKRS TAX_OFF SEQNO D_SEQNO.

    LOOP AT B_RECORD WHERE BUKRS = A_RECORD-BUKRS.
      PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_04 USING 'B' FILE_TYPE.
      LOOP AT C_RECORD_HON WHERE TAX_OFF = B_RECORD-TAX_OFF
                             AND STCD2   = B_RECORD-STCD2
                             AND BUKRS   = B_RECORD-BUKRS.
        PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_04 USING 'C' FILE_TYPE.
        LOOP AT D_RECORD  WHERE PERNR = C_RECORD_HON-PERNR.
          PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_04 USING 'D' FILE_TYPE.
        ENDLOOP.
      ENDLOOP.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM. " write_to_temse_2004

*&      Form  WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_04

*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_5717   text
*      -->P_FILE_TYPE  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_04 USING RECORDNAME FILE_TYPE. "QU796286
  IF FILE_TYPE = 'LSP'.
    CLEAR DATAFILE. REFRESH DATAFILE.

    CASE RECORDNAME.
      WHEN 'A'.
        PERFORM  ADD_ARECORD_TO_DATAFILE_04.
      WHEN 'B'.
        PERFORM  ADD_BRECORD_TO_DATAFILE_01.
      WHEN 'C'.
        PERFORM  ADD_CRECORD_TO_DATAFILE_04.
      WHEN 'D'.
        PERFORM  ADD_DRECORD_TO_DATAFILE_01.
    ENDCASE.
*    APPEND DATAFILE.   "SHAO796286
    CALL FUNCTION 'RSTS_WRITE'
      EXPORTING
        FBHANDLE = OBJHD
      TABLES
        DATATAB  = DATAFILE.
  ELSEIF FILE_TYPE = 'HON'.
    CLEAR DATAFILE_HON. REFRESH DATAFILE_HON.

    CASE RECORDNAME.
      WHEN 'A'.
        PERFORM  ADD_ARECORD_TO_DATAFILE_HON_04.
      WHEN 'B'.
        PERFORM  ADD_BRECORD_TO_DATAFILE_HON_04.
      WHEN 'C'.
        PERFORM  ADD_CRECORD_TO_DATAFILE_HON_04.
      WHEN 'D'.
        PERFORM  ADD_DRECORD_TO_DATAFILE_HON_04.
    ENDCASE.
*    APPEND DATAFILE_HON.   "SHAO796286
    CALL FUNCTION 'RSTS_WRITE'
      EXPORTING
        FBHANDLE = OBJHD
      TABLES
        DATATAB  = DATAFILE_HON.

  ENDIF.

ENDFORM. " WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_04

FORM ADD_ARECORD_TO_DATAFILE_04 . "QU796286
  MOVE :   A_RECORD-R_MODIF        TO DATAFILE+0(1),
           A_RECORD-D_MODIF        TO DATAFILE+1(2),
           A_RECORD-TAX_OFF        TO DATAFILE+3(3),
           A_RECORD-SUBMITDT       TO DATAFILE+6(8),
           A_RECORD-AGENCY         TO DATAFILE+14(1),
           A_RECORD-AGENT_NUM      TO DATAFILE+15(6),
           A_RECORD-HOME_TAX_ID    TO DATAFILE+21(20),   "Home tax ID
           A_RECORD-ITEM8_SPACE    TO DATAFILE+41(4),       " '9000'
           A_RECORD-STCD2          TO DATAFILE+45(10),
           A_RECORD-COMNM          TO DATAFILE+55(40),
           A_RECORD-SUB_DEPT       TO DATAFILE+95(30),   "Dept. of sub
           A_RECORD-SUB_NAME       TO DATAFILE+125(30),  "Name of sub
*          A_RECORD-STCD1          TO DATAFILE+71(13),
*          A_RECORD-REPRES         TO DATAFILE+84(30),
           A_RECORD-AG_TELNR       TO DATAFILE+155(15),
           A_RECORD-B_REC_NUM      TO DATAFILE+170(5),
           A_RECORD-HANGLE_CODE    TO DATAFILE+175(3),
           A_RECORD-TARGET_PRD     TO DATAFILE+178(1),
*          A_RECORD-CORRECTION     TO DATAFILE+138(1),
           A_RECORD-ASPACE         TO DATAFILE+179(151).
* Unicode SHAO796286
  IF CL_ABAP_CHAR_UTILITIES=>CHARSIZE > 1.
    CLEAR  OUT_XSTRING.  CLEAR  ADJ_STRING. CLEAR  ADJ_XSTRING.
* Convert unicode string to hex stream
    PERFORM UNISTRING_TO_XSTRING USING   DATAFILE+0(95)  95.
    MOVE OUT_XSTRING TO  ADJ_XSTRING.

    PERFORM UNISTRING_TO_XSTRING USING   DATAFILE+95(30) 30.
    CONCATENATE ADJ_XSTRING OUT_XSTRING INTO ADJ_XSTRING IN BYTE MODE.

    PERFORM UNISTRING_TO_XSTRING USING   DATAFILE+125(30) 30.
    CONCATENATE ADJ_XSTRING OUT_XSTRING INTO ADJ_XSTRING IN BYTE MODE.

    PERFORM UNISTRING_TO_XSTRING USING   DATAFILE+155(175) 175.
    CONCATENATE ADJ_XSTRING OUT_XSTRING INTO ADJ_XSTRING IN BYTE MODE.
* Comvert hex stream to unicode string
    PERFORM  XSTRING_TO_UNISTRING.
    CLEAR DATAFILE. REFRESH DATAFILE.
    APPEND ADJ_STRING TO DATAFILE.
* Unicode end
  ELSE.
    APPEND DATAFILE.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM. " add_arecord_to_datafile_04

FORM FILL_D_RECORD_HON .
  DATA :   NUM1         TYPE  I,
           NUM2         TYPE  I,
           BETRG_C(14)  TYPE  C,
           BETRG_N(14)  TYPE  N,
           TEMP_BIZ_NO  LIKE  INCOME-BUS01. "BAIW1025771
  CLEAR D_RECORD.

  NUM2  = 0.
  LOOP AT INCOME WHERE SEQNO > 0 AND PERNR = PERNR-PERNR.
    NUM2 = NUM2 + 1.              "Num of records(per employee)

    MOVE C_RECORD_HON-BUKRS   TO D_RECORD-BUKRS.

           '22'                 TO D_RECORD-D_MODIF,

           SPACE                TO D_RECORD-PRE_SPACE,
           C_RECORD_HON-REGNO   TO D_RECORD-REGNO,
*     Company name and
           INCOME-COM01       TO D_RECORD-COM02,
*     Business number
           INCOME-BUS01       TO TEMP_BIZ_NO. "BAIW1025771

*BAIW1025771 Begin clear symbol - in biz.no.

      REPLACE '-'  WITH '' INTO TEMP_BIZ_NO.
      IF SY-SUBRC <> 0.
        EXIT.
      ENDIF.
    ENDDO.

    CONDENSE TEMP_BIZ_NO NO-GAPS.

    MOVE TEMP_BIZ_NO TO D_RECORD-BUS02.
*BAIW1025771 END

    MOVE   INCOME-SPY01       TO BETRG_C.
    MOVE   BETRG_C            TO D_RECORD-SPY02.

    MOVE   INCOME-HPY01       TO BETRG_C.
    MOVE   BETRG_C            TO D_RECORD-HPY02.

    MOVE   INCOME-IPY01       TO BETRG_C.
    MOVE   BETRG_C            TO D_RECORD-IPY02.

    MOVE   BETRG_C            TO D_RECORD-TOT02.

           SPACE              TO D_RECORD-CORRECTION,
           SPACE              TO D_RECORD-DSPACE.

* SHAO796286

    MOVE   '25'   TO D_RECORD-D_MODIF_HON.

*<<< N1432309: Previous - Ret. Reason; Exeption Ind.; Service period
    MOVE   '5'                TO D_RECORD-RET_RSN.
*    IF sy-index = 1.                                   "N1563352
    IF NUM2 = 1.                                            "N1563352
      IF ITAB_C-TXEMP01 = 'O'.
        D_RECORD-EXEMPT_IND = '1'.
      ELSEIF ITAB_C-N_TXEMP01 = 'O'.
        D_RECORD-EXEMPT_IND = '2'.
      ENDIF.
      "<<< N1536031

      ENDIF.

FORM TRUNC USING DIGIT
           CHANGING VALUE.
  VALUE = VALUE * 100.
  VALUE = VALUE / ( 10 ** DIGIT ).
  VALUE = TRUNC( VALUE ).
  VALUE = VALUE * ( 10 ** DIGIT ).
  VALUE = VALUE / 100.
ENDFORM. " trunc

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  ERROR_HANDLING
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->iv_msgty
*      -->iv_free_text
*      -->iv_msgv3
*      -->iv_msgv4
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       To add message to application log
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*

FORM ERROR_HANDLING2 USING IV_MSGTY TYPE SY-MSGTY
                             IV_FREE_TEXT TYPE C
                             IV_MSGV3 TYPE SY-MSGV3
                             IV_MSGV4 TYPE SY-MSGV4.

  DATA: LV_MSGID TYPE SY-MSGID VALUE 'BL',
        LV_MSGNO TYPE SY-MSGNO VALUE '001'.

  DATA: LV_FREE_TEXT(200) TYPE C, "Variable to store messages
        LT_TLINE TYPE TABLE OF TLINE,
        LS_TLINE TYPE TLINE.

  DATA: BEGIN OF LS_MSG_TXT,
          MSGV1 TYPE SY-MSGV1,
          MSGV2 TYPE SY-MSGV2,
          MSGV3 TYPE SY-MSGV3,
          MSGV4 TYPE SY-MSGV4,
          END OF LS_MSG_TXT.

**Assign message txt
  MOVE IV_FREE_TEXT TO LS_MSG_TXT.


  IF STRLEN( IV_FREE_TEXT ) > 50.
    MOVE IV_FREE_TEXT TO LS_TLINE-TDLINE.
    APPEND LS_TLINE TO LT_TLINE.
    CALL FUNCTION 'FORMAT_TEXTLINES'
      EXPORTING
        FORMATWIDTH = 50
        LINEWIDTH   = 134
        STARTLINE   = 0
      TABLES
        LINES       = LT_TLINE
      EXCEPTIONS
        OTHERS      = 0.
    DO 4 TIMES.
      READ TABLE LT_TLINE INTO LS_TLINE INDEX SY-INDEX.
      IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
        CASE SY-INDEX.
          WHEN 1.
            MOVE LS_TLINE-TDLINE TO LS_MSG_TXT-MSGV1.
          WHEN 2.
            MOVE LS_TLINE-TDLINE TO LS_MSG_TXT-MSGV2.
          WHEN 3.
            MOVE LS_TLINE-TDLINE TO LS_MSG_TXT-MSGV3.
          WHEN 4.
            MOVE LS_TLINE-TDLINE TO LS_MSG_TXT-MSGV4.
        ENDCASE.
      ENDIF.
    ENDDO.
  ELSE.
    LS_MSG_TXT-MSGV1 = IV_FREE_TEXT.
  ENDIF.

  MESSAGE ID LV_MSGID TYPE IV_MSGTY
    NUMBER LV_MSGNO
    WITH LS_MSG_TXT-MSGV1 LS_MSG_TXT-MSGV2 IV_MSGV3 IV_MSGV4
    INTO OBJ_PAL_LOG->G_MSG_TEXT.
  OBJ_PAL_LOG->CREATE_PERNR_MSG(                          "ACC
    P_PERNR = PERNR-PERNR ).
  CALL FUNCTION 'HR_PAL_GENRL_MSG_ADD'
    EXPORTING
      I_MSGID = LV_MSGID
      I_MSGTY = IV_MSGTY
      I_MSGNO = LV_MSGNO
      I_MSGV1 = LS_MSG_TXT-MSGV1
      I_MSGV2 = LS_MSG_TXT-MSGV2
      I_MSGV3 = IV_MSGV3
      I_MSGV4 = IV_MSGV4.

ENDFORM. "error_handling1

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Form Create_alv_header
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*    <--> cr_content
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
* To create ALV header.
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*

FORM CREATE_ALV_HEADER
                   CHANGING CR_CONTENT TYPE REF TO CL_SALV_FORM_ELEMENT
.

  DATA: LV_TEXT1 TYPE STRING,
        LR_LABEL  TYPE REF TO CL_SALV_FORM_LABEL.           "#EC NEEDED


  IF NOT GT_OUTTAB3 IS INITIAL.
    MOVE TEXT-L33 TO LV_TEXT1.
  ENDIF.

  IF NOT GT_OUTTAB4 IS INITIAL AND GV_FLAG = 'Y'.
    MOVE TEXT-L34 TO LV_TEXT1.
  ENDIF.

  IF NOT GT_OUTTAB5 IS INITIAL AND GV_FLAG = 'Z'.
    MOVE TEXT-L35 TO LV_TEXT1.
  ENDIF.

  CREATE OBJECT GR_GRID.

  LR_LABEL =  GR_GRID->CREATE_LABEL(
             ROW         = 1
             COLUMN      = 1
             TEXT        = LV_TEXT1 ).

  CR_CONTENT = GR_GRID.

ENDFORM. "create_alv_header

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  write_to_temse_2006
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Note 1018000 For YEA2006
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->FILE_TYPE  LSP or HON?
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_2006 USING FILE_TYPE.
  CHECK TEMSE_OPEN = TRUE.
  IF FILE_TYPE = 'LSP'.
    PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_06 USING 'A' FILE_TYPE.

    SORT B_RECORD BY BUKRS SEQNO.
    SORT C_RECORD BY BUKRS TAX_OFF STCD2 SEQNO.
    SORT D_RECORD BY BUKRS TAX_OFF SEQNO D_SEQNO.

    LOOP AT B_RECORD WHERE BUKRS = A_RECORD-BUKRS.
      PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_06 USING 'B' FILE_TYPE.
      LOOP AT C_RECORD WHERE TAX_OFF = B_RECORD-TAX_OFF
                         AND STCD2   = B_RECORD-STCD2
                         AND BUKRS   = B_RECORD-BUKRS.
        PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_06 USING 'C' FILE_TYPE.
        LOOP AT D_RECORD  WHERE PERNR = C_RECORD-PERNR.
          PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_06 USING 'D' FILE_TYPE.
        ENDLOOP.
      ENDLOOP.
    ENDLOOP.
  ELSEIF FILE_TYPE = 'HON'.
    PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_06 USING 'A' FILE_TYPE.

    SORT B_RECORD     BY BUKRS SEQNO.
    SORT C_RECORD_HON BY BUKRS TAX_OFF STCD2 SEQNO.
    SORT D_RECORD     BY BUKRS TAX_OFF SEQNO D_SEQNO.

    LOOP AT B_RECORD WHERE BUKRS = A_RECORD-BUKRS.
      PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_06 USING 'B' FILE_TYPE.
      LOOP AT C_RECORD_HON WHERE TAX_OFF = B_RECORD-TAX_OFF
                             AND STCD2   = B_RECORD-STCD2
                             AND BUKRS   = B_RECORD-BUKRS.
        PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_06 USING 'C' FILE_TYPE.
        LOOP AT D_RECORD  WHERE PERNR = C_RECORD_HON-PERNR.
          PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_06 USING 'D' FILE_TYPE.
        ENDLOOP.
      ENDLOOP.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM. " write_to_temse_2006
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_06
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Note 1018000 For YEA2006
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->recordname   name of record (A/B/C/D)
*      -->file_type    LSP or HON?
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_06 USING RECORDNAME FILE_TYPE.
  IF FILE_TYPE = 'LSP'.
    CLEAR DATAFILE_06. REFRESH DATAFILE_06.

    CASE RECORDNAME.
      WHEN 'A'.
        PERFORM  ADD_ARECORD_TO_DATAFILE_06.
      WHEN 'B'.
        PERFORM  ADD_BRECORD_TO_DATAFILE_06.
      WHEN 'C'.
        PERFORM  ADD_CRECORD_TO_DATAFILE_06.
      WHEN 'D'.
        PERFORM  ADD_DRECORD_TO_DATAFILE_06.
    ENDCASE.
    CALL FUNCTION 'RSTS_WRITE'
      EXPORTING
        FBHANDLE = OBJHD
      TABLES
        DATATAB  = DATAFILE_06.
  ELSEIF FILE_TYPE = 'HON'.
    CLEAR DATAFILE_HON06. REFRESH DATAFILE_HON06.

    CASE RECORDNAME.
      WHEN 'A'.
        PERFORM  ADD_ARECORD_TO_DATAFILE_HON_06.
      WHEN 'B'.
        PERFORM  ADD_BRECORD_TO_DATAFILE_HON_06.
      WHEN 'C'.
        PERFORM  ADD_CRECORD_TO_DATAFILE_HON_06.
      WHEN 'D'.
        PERFORM  ADD_DRECORD_TO_DATAFILE_HON_06.
    ENDCASE.
    CALL FUNCTION 'RSTS_WRITE'
      EXPORTING
        FBHANDLE = OBJHD
      TABLES
        DATATAB  = DATAFILE_HON06.

  ENDIF.

ENDFORM. " WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_06
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  add_arecord_to_datafile_06
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Note 1018000 For YEA2006
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM ADD_ARECORD_TO_DATAFILE_06 .
  MOVE :   A_RECORD-R_MODIF        TO DATAFILE_06+0(1),
           A_RECORD-D_MODIF        TO DATAFILE_06+1(2),
           A_RECORD-TAX_OFF        TO DATAFILE_06+3(3),
           A_RECORD-SUBMITDT       TO DATAFILE_06+6(8),
           A_RECORD-AGENCY         TO DATAFILE_06+14(1),
           A_RECORD-AGENT_NUM      TO DATAFILE_06+15(6),
           A_RECORD-HOME_TAX_ID    TO DATAFILE_06+21(20),   "Home tax ID
           A_RECORD-ITEM8_SPACE    TO DATAFILE_06+41(4),    " '9000'
           A_RECORD-STCD2          TO DATAFILE_06+45(10),
           A_RECORD-COMNM          TO DATAFILE_06+55(40),
           A_RECORD-SUB_DEPT       TO DATAFILE_06+95(30),   "Dept. of sub
           A_RECORD-SUB_NAME       TO DATAFILE_06+125(30),  "Name of sub
           A_RECORD-AG_TELNR       TO DATAFILE_06+155(15),
           A_RECORD-B_REC_NUM      TO DATAFILE_06+170(5),
           A_RECORD-HANGLE_CODE    TO DATAFILE_06+175(3),
           A_RECORD-TARGET_PRD     TO DATAFILE_06+178(1),
           A_RECORD-ASPACE         TO DATAFILE_06+179(281).
* Unicode SHAO796268
  IF CL_ABAP_CHAR_UTILITIES=>CHARSIZE > 1.
    CLEAR  OUT_XSTRING.  CLEAR  ADJ_STRING. CLEAR  ADJ_XSTRING.
* Convert unicode string to hex stream
    PERFORM UNISTRING_TO_XSTRING USING   DATAFILE_06+0(95)  95.
    MOVE OUT_XSTRING TO  ADJ_XSTRING.

    PERFORM UNISTRING_TO_XSTRING USING   DATAFILE_06+95(30) 30.
    CONCATENATE ADJ_XSTRING OUT_XSTRING INTO ADJ_XSTRING IN BYTE MODE.

    PERFORM UNISTRING_TO_XSTRING USING   DATAFILE_06+125(30) 30.
    CONCATENATE ADJ_XSTRING OUT_XSTRING INTO ADJ_XSTRING IN BYTE MODE.

    PERFORM UNISTRING_TO_XSTRING USING   DATAFILE_06+155(305) 305.
    CONCATENATE ADJ_XSTRING OUT_XSTRING INTO ADJ_XSTRING IN BYTE MODE.
* Comvert hex stream to unicode string
    PERFORM  XSTRING_TO_UNISTRING.
    CLEAR DATAFILE_06. REFRESH DATAFILE_06.
    APPEND ADJ_STRING TO DATAFILE_06.
* Unicode end
  ELSE.
    APPEND DATAFILE_06.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM. " add_arecord_to_datafile_06

            C_RECORD-SVR_MONTH       TO  DATAFILE_06+273(4),
            C_RECORD-EXC_MONTH       TO  DATAFILE_06+277(4),
            C_RECORD-PRE_ENTDT       TO  DATAFILE_06+281(8),
            C_RECORD-PRE_RSGNDT      TO  DATAFILE_06+289(8),
            C_RECORD-PRE_SVR         TO  DATAFILE_06+297(4),
            C_RECORD-PRE_EXCMONTH    TO  DATAFILE_06+301(4),
            C_RECORD-DUP_MONTH       TO  DATAFILE_06+305(4),
            C_RECORD-TAX_SPERIOD     TO  DATAFILE_06+309(2),

            C_RECORD-SEP_AMT         TO  DATAFILE_06+311(10),
            C_RECORD-SEP_DEDUCT      TO  DATAFILE_06+321(10),
            C_RECORD-TAXBASE         TO  DATAFILE_06+331(10),

            C_RECORD-TAXB_YEAR       TO  DATAFILE_06+341(10),
            C_RECORD-TAX_YEAR        TO  DATAFILE_06+351(10),
            C_RECORD-CTAX            TO  DATAFILE_06+361(10),

            C_RECORD-DET_ITAX        TO  DATAFILE_06+371(10),
            C_RECORD-DET_RTAX        TO  DATAFILE_06+381(10),
            C_RECORD-DET_STAX        TO  DATAFILE_06+391(10),
            C_RECORD-DET_TOTAL1      TO  DATAFILE_06+401(10),

            C_RECORD-PRE_ITAX        TO  DATAFILE_06+411(10),
            C_RECORD-PRE_RTAX        TO  DATAFILE_06+421(10),
            C_RECORD-PRE_STAX        TO  DATAFILE_06+431(10),
            C_RECORD-PRE_TOTAL1      TO  DATAFILE_06+441(10),
            C_RECORD-SPACE_3         TO  DATAFILE_06+451(9).
* Unicode SHAO796286
  IF CL_ABAP_CHAR_UTILITIES=>CHARSIZE > 1.
    CLEAR  OUT_XSTRING.  CLEAR  ADJ_STRING. CLEAR  ADJ_XSTRING.
* Convert unicode string to hex stream
    PERFORM UNISTRING_TO_XSTRING USING   DATAFILE_06+0(73)  73.
    MOVE OUT_XSTRING TO  ADJ_XSTRING.

    PERFORM UNISTRING_TO_XSTRING USING   DATAFILE_06+73(387) 387.
    CONCATENATE ADJ_XSTRING OUT_XSTRING INTO ADJ_XSTRING IN BYTE MODE.

* Comvert hex stream to unicode string
    PERFORM  XSTRING_TO_UNISTRING.
    CLEAR DATAFILE_06. REFRESH DATAFILE_06.
    APPEND ADJ_STRING TO DATAFILE_06.
* Unicode end
  ELSE.
    APPEND DATAFILE_06.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM. " add_crecord_to_datafile_06

*<<< JINQUE1289311 YEA08
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  WRITE_TO_TEMSE_2008
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text: note1289311
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_FILE_TYPE  LSP OR HON?
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_2008 USING FILE_TYPE.
  CHECK TEMSE_OPEN = TRUE.
  IF FILE_TYPE = 'LSP'.
    PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_08 USING 'A' FILE_TYPE.

    SORT B_RECORD BY BUKRS SEQNO.
    SORT C_RECORD BY BUKRS TAX_OFF STCD2 SEQNO.
    SORT D_RECORD BY BUKRS TAX_OFF SEQNO D_SEQNO.

    LOOP AT B_RECORD WHERE BUKRS = A_RECORD-BUKRS.
      PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_08 USING 'B' FILE_TYPE.
      LOOP AT C_RECORD WHERE TAX_OFF = B_RECORD-TAX_OFF
                         AND STCD2   = B_RECORD-STCD2
                         AND BUKRS   = B_RECORD-BUKRS.
        PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_08 USING 'C' FILE_TYPE.
        LOOP AT D_RECORD  WHERE PERNR = C_RECORD-PERNR.
          PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_08 USING 'D' FILE_TYPE.
        ENDLOOP.
      ENDLOOP.
    ENDLOOP.
  ELSEIF FILE_TYPE = 'HON'.
    PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_08 USING 'A' FILE_TYPE.

    SORT B_RECORD     BY BUKRS SEQNO.
    SORT C_RECORD_HON BY BUKRS TAX_OFF STCD2 SEQNO.
    SORT D_RECORD     BY BUKRS TAX_OFF SEQNO D_SEQNO.

    LOOP AT B_RECORD WHERE BUKRS = A_RECORD-BUKRS.
      PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_08 USING 'B' FILE_TYPE.
      LOOP AT C_RECORD_HON WHERE TAX_OFF = B_RECORD-TAX_OFF
                             AND STCD2   = B_RECORD-STCD2
                             AND BUKRS   = B_RECORD-BUKRS.
        PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_08 USING 'C' FILE_TYPE.
        LOOP AT D_RECORD  WHERE PERNR = C_RECORD_HON-PERNR.
          PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_08 USING 'D' FILE_TYPE.
        ENDLOOP.
      ENDLOOP.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM. " WRITE_TO_TEMSE_2008
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_08
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text: note1289311
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->RECORDNAME   NAME OF RECORD (A/B/C/D)
*      -->FILE_TYPE    LSP OR HON?
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_08 USING RECORDNAME
                                      FILE_TYPE.
  IF FILE_TYPE = 'LSP'.
    CLEAR DATAFILE_08. REFRESH DATAFILE_08.

    CASE RECORDNAME.
      WHEN 'A'.
        PERFORM  ADD_ARECORD_TO_DATAFILE_08.
      WHEN 'B'.
        PERFORM  ADD_BRECORD_TO_DATAFILE_08.
      WHEN 'C'.
        PERFORM  ADD_CRECORD_TO_DATAFILE_08.
      WHEN 'D'.
        PERFORM  ADD_DRECORD_TO_DATAFILE_08.
    ENDCASE.
    CALL FUNCTION 'RSTS_WRITE'
      EXPORTING
        FBHANDLE = OBJHD
      TABLES
        DATATAB  = DATAFILE_08.
  ELSEIF FILE_TYPE = 'HON'.
    CLEAR DATAFILE_HON08. REFRESH DATAFILE_HON08.

    CASE RECORDNAME.
      WHEN 'A'.
        PERFORM  ADD_ARECORD_TO_DATAFILE_HON_08.
      WHEN 'B'.
        PERFORM  ADD_BRECORD_TO_DATAFILE_HON_08.
      WHEN 'C'.
        PERFORM  ADD_CRECORD_TO_DATAFILE_HON_08.
      WHEN 'D'.
        PERFORM  ADD_DRECORD_TO_DATAFILE_HON_08.
    ENDCASE.
    CALL FUNCTION 'RSTS_WRITE'
      EXPORTING
        FBHANDLE = OBJHD
      TABLES
        DATATAB  = DATAFILE_HON08.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM. " WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_08

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  RE511K_WITHOUT_DUMP
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       guost N1325470
*       When no value, just clear t511k-kwert without error message
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_41     text
*      -->P_1437   text
*      -->P_KRSP1_SEPDT  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM RE511K_WITHOUT_DUMP USING
                           $MOLGA LIKE T500L-MOLGA
                           $KONST TYPE C
                           $DATUM LIKE SY-DATUM.

  CHECK T511K-MOLGA NE $MOLGA OR T511K-KONST NE $KONST OR
        T511K-BEGDA GT $DATUM OR T511K-ENDDA LT $DATUM.   "#EC PORTABLE
  SELECT * FROM T511K WHERE MOLGA EQ $MOLGA
                        AND KONST EQ $KONST
                        AND BEGDA LE $DATUM
                        AND ENDDA GE $DATUM.              "#EC PORTABLE
  ENDSELECT.
  IF SY-SUBRC NE 0.
*   CLEAR t511k-kwert.
    CLEAR T511K.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM. "re511k_without_dump

*N1345462 YANGQ Begin.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  FILL_RETIRE_REASON
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM FILL_RETIRE_REASON .
  DATA RET_ID TYPE I.
  IF NOT KRSP1-RETID IS INITIAL.
    RET_ID = KRSP1-RETID.
  ELSE.
    LOOP AT P0538 WHERE SEPDT = KRSP1-SEPDT.
      RET_ID = P0538-RETID.
      EXIT.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDIF.
  IF NOT RET_ID IS INITIAL.
    PERFORM FILL_REASON_BY_ID USING RET_ID.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM. " FILL_RETIRE_REASON
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  FILL_REASON_BY_ID
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_RET_ID  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM FILL_REASON_BY_ID USING P_RET_ID.

  DATA L_INDEX(2)            TYPE N.
  DATA L_VAR_NAME            TYPE FIELDNAME.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS  <L_IF_NAME> TYPE ANY.

  L_INDEX = P_RET_ID.
  CONCATENATE
  'employee-ret_res'
  L_INDEX
  INTO L_VAR_NAME.

  ASSIGN (L_VAR_NAME) TO <L_IF_NAME>.

  IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
    <L_IF_NAME> = 'X'.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM. " FILL_REASON_BY_ID
*N1345462 YANGQ End.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  WRITE_TO_TEMSE_2009
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*[Log]  Create: N1432309
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->FILE_TYPE  LSP OR HON?
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_2009 USING FILE_TYPE.
  CHECK TEMSE_OPEN = TRUE.
  IF FILE_TYPE = 'LSP'.
    PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_09 USING 'A' FILE_TYPE.

    SORT B_RECORD BY BUKRS SEQNO.
    SORT C_RECORD BY BUKRS TAX_OFF STCD2 SEQNO.
    SORT D_RECORD BY BUKRS TAX_OFF SEQNO D_SEQNO.

    LOOP AT B_RECORD WHERE BUKRS = A_RECORD-BUKRS.
      PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_09 USING 'B' FILE_TYPE.
      LOOP AT C_RECORD WHERE TAX_OFF = B_RECORD-TAX_OFF
                         AND STCD2   = B_RECORD-STCD2
                         AND BUKRS   = B_RECORD-BUKRS.
        PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_09 USING 'C' FILE_TYPE.
        LOOP AT D_RECORD  WHERE PERNR = C_RECORD-PERNR.
          PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_09 USING 'D' FILE_TYPE.
        ENDLOOP.
      ENDLOOP.
    ENDLOOP.
  ELSEIF FILE_TYPE = 'HON'.
    PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_09 USING 'A' FILE_TYPE.

    SORT B_RECORD     BY BUKRS SEQNO.
    SORT C_RECORD_HON BY BUKRS TAX_OFF STCD2 SEQNO.
    SORT D_RECORD     BY BUKRS TAX_OFF SEQNO D_SEQNO.

    LOOP AT B_RECORD WHERE BUKRS = A_RECORD-BUKRS.
      PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_09 USING 'B' FILE_TYPE.
      LOOP AT C_RECORD_HON WHERE TAX_OFF = B_RECORD-TAX_OFF
                             AND STCD2   = B_RECORD-STCD2
                             AND BUKRS   = B_RECORD-BUKRS.
        PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_09 USING 'C' FILE_TYPE.
        LOOP AT D_RECORD  WHERE PERNR = C_RECORD_HON-PERNR.
          PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_09 USING 'D' FILE_TYPE.
        ENDLOOP.
      ENDLOOP.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM. " WRITE_TO_TEMSE_2009
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_09
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*[Log]  Create: N1432309
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->RECORDNAME   NAME OF RECORD (A/B/C/D)
*      -->FILE_TYPE    LSP OR HON?
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_09 USING RECORDNAME
                                      FILE_TYPE.
  IF FILE_TYPE = 'LSP'.
    CLEAR DATAFILE_09. REFRESH DATAFILE_09.

    CASE RECORDNAME.
      WHEN 'A'.
        PERFORM  ADD_ARECORD_TO_DATAFILE_09.
      WHEN 'B'.
        PERFORM  ADD_BRECORD_TO_DATAFILE_09.
      WHEN 'C'.
        PERFORM  ADD_CRECORD_TO_DATAFILE_09.
      WHEN 'D'.
        PERFORM  ADD_DRECORD_TO_DATAFILE_09.
    ENDCASE.
    CALL FUNCTION 'RSTS_WRITE'
      EXPORTING
        FBHANDLE = OBJHD
      TABLES
        DATATAB  = DATAFILE_09.
  ELSEIF FILE_TYPE = 'HON'.
    CLEAR DATAFILE_HON09. REFRESH DATAFILE_HON09.

    CASE RECORDNAME.
      WHEN 'A'.
        PERFORM  ADD_ARECORD_TO_DATAFILE_HON_09.
      WHEN 'B'.
        PERFORM  ADD_BRECORD_TO_DATAFILE_HON_09.
      WHEN 'C'.
        PERFORM  ADD_CRECORD_TO_DATAFILE_HON_09.
      WHEN 'D'.
        PERFORM  ADD_DRECORD_TO_DATAFILE_HON_09.
    ENDCASE.
    CALL FUNCTION 'RSTS_WRITE'
      EXPORTING
        FBHANDLE = OBJHD
      TABLES
        DATATAB  = DATAFILE_HON09.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM. " WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_09
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  ADD_ARECORD_TO_DATAFILE_09
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*[Log]  Create: N1432309
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM ADD_ARECORD_TO_DATAFILE_09 .
  MOVE :   A_RECORD-R_MODIF        TO DATAFILE_09+0(1),
           A_RECORD-D_MODIF        TO DATAFILE_09+1(2),
           A_RECORD-TAX_OFF        TO DATAFILE_09+3(3),
           A_RECORD-SUBMITDT       TO DATAFILE_09+6(8),
           A_RECORD-AGENCY         TO DATAFILE_09+14(1),
           A_RECORD-AGENT_NUM      TO DATAFILE_09+15(6),
           A_RECORD-HOME_TAX_ID    TO DATAFILE_09+21(20),
           A_RECORD-ITEM8_SPACE    TO DATAFILE_09+41(4),
           A_RECORD-STCD2          TO DATAFILE_09+45(10),
           A_RECORD-COMNM          TO DATAFILE_09+55(40),
           A_RECORD-SUB_DEPT       TO DATAFILE_09+95(30),
           A_RECORD-SUB_NAME       TO DATAFILE_09+125(30),
           A_RECORD-AG_TELNR       TO DATAFILE_09+155(15),
           A_RECORD-B_REC_NUM      TO DATAFILE_09+170(5),
           A_RECORD-HANGLE_CODE    TO DATAFILE_09+175(3),
           SPACE                   TO DATAFILE_09+178(342).

* Unicode SHAO796268
  IF CL_ABAP_CHAR_UTILITIES=>CHARSIZE > 1.
    CLEAR  OUT_XSTRING.  CLEAR  ADJ_STRING. CLEAR  ADJ_XSTRING.
* Convert unicode string to hex stream
    PERFORM UNISTRING_TO_XSTRING USING   DATAFILE_09+0(95)  95.
    MOVE OUT_XSTRING TO  ADJ_XSTRING.

    PERFORM UNISTRING_TO_XSTRING USING   DATAFILE_09+95(30) 30.
    CONCATENATE ADJ_XSTRING OUT_XSTRING INTO ADJ_XSTRING IN BYTE MODE.

    PERFORM UNISTRING_TO_XSTRING USING   DATAFILE_09+125(30) 30.
    CONCATENATE ADJ_XSTRING OUT_XSTRING INTO ADJ_XSTRING IN BYTE MODE.

    PERFORM UNISTRING_TO_XSTRING USING   DATAFILE_09+155(365) 365.
    CONCATENATE ADJ_XSTRING OUT_XSTRING INTO ADJ_XSTRING IN BYTE MODE.
* Comvert hex stream to unicode string
    PERFORM  XSTRING_TO_UNISTRING.
    CLEAR DATAFILE_09. REFRESH DATAFILE_09.
    APPEND ADJ_STRING TO DATAFILE_09.
* Unicode end
  ELSE.
    APPEND DATAFILE_09.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM. " ADD_ARECORD_TO_DATAFILE_09

*---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       FORM get_default_seal        Print company seal on the form
*---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM GET_DEFAULT_SEAL.

  TABLES:
    PME04.

  DATA:
    BEGIN OF ECC_TAB OCCURS 10,
      BACK(100) TYPE C,
    END OF ECC_TAB.

  DATA:
    LV_FORM_NAME(20) TYPE C,
    LV_SEAL_NAME(80) TYPE C,
    LV_BACK(100)     TYPE C,
    LV_STRUC(5)      TYPE C.

  FIELD-SYMBOLS:

    <FS_STRUC_COTNENT_SET>,
    <FS_STRUC_CONTENT>.

  CONSTANTS:
    LC_FIELD_NAME_BUKRS TYPE FIELDNAME   VALUE 'BUKRS',
    LC_SEPERATION       TYPE C           VALUE '/',
    LC_FEATURE_NAME     TYPE T549B-NAMEN VALUE '41SEA'.

  CHECK CB_SEAL IS INITIAL.
  CHECK GV_PRE_COMPANY_CODE <> PERNR-BUKRS.

  CLEAR GV_COM_SEAL.
  CLEAR LV_BACK.
  MOVE PERNR-BUKRS TO GV_PRE_COMPANY_CODE.

  SELECT SINGLE STRUC FROM  T549D
                      INTO  LV_STRUC
                      WHERE NAMEN = LC_FEATURE_NAME.

  CHECK SY-SUBRC EQ 0.

  ASSIGN (LV_STRUC) TO <FS_STRUC_CONTENT>.
  CHECK <FS_STRUC_CONTENT> IS ASSIGNED.
  ASSIGN COMPONENT LC_FIELD_NAME_BUKRS OF STRUCTURE <FS_STRUC_CONTENT>
                                       TO <FS_STRUC_COTNENT_SET>.
  MOVE PERNR-BUKRS TO <FS_STRUC_CONTENT>.

  CALL FUNCTION 'HR_FEATURE_BACKTABLE'
    EXPORTING
      FEATURE       = LC_FEATURE_NAME
      STRUC_CONTENT = <FS_STRUC_CONTENT>
      KIND_OF_ERROR = '3'
    TABLES
      BACK          = ECC_TAB
    EXCEPTIONS
      OTHERS        = 1.

  CHECK SY-SUBRC = 0.

  LOOP AT ECC_TAB.
    CHECK ECC_TAB-BACK IS NOT INITIAL.

    SPLIT ECC_TAB-BACK AT LC_SEPERATION INTO LV_FORM_NAME LV_SEAL_NAME.
    IF LV_FORM_NAME IS INITIAL.
      MOVE LV_SEAL_NAME TO GV_COM_SEAL.
    ELSE.
      CHECK LV_FORM_NAME = SY-REPID.
      MOVE LV_SEAL_NAME TO GV_COM_SEAL.
      EXIT.
    ENDIF.

  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM. "get_default_seal
* End of note 1434166

*&      Form  FILL_SP_2010
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM FILL_SP_2010.
  DATA: AMOUNT          LIKE RT-BETRG,
        TOTAL           LIKE RT-BETRG,
        TMP_TTL         LIKE RT-BETRG,
        SEPAR_CUR       LIKE RT-BETRG,
        HONOR_CUR       LIKE RT-BETRG,
        SEPAR_PRE1      LIKE RT-BETRG,
        HONOR_PRE1      LIKE RT-BETRG,
        SEPAR_PRE2      LIKE RT-BETRG,
        HONOR_PRE2      LIKE RT-BETRG,
        SEPRP_CUR       LIKE RT-BETRG,
        HONRP_CUR       LIKE RT-BETRG,
        SEPRP_PRE       LIKE RT-BETRG,
        HONRP_PRE       LIKE RT-BETRG,
        SEPAR_DEP_CUR   LIKE RT-BETRG,
        HONOR_DEP_CUR   LIKE RT-BETRG,
        SEPAR_DEP_PRE   LIKE RT-BETRG,
        HONOR_DEP_PRE   LIKE RT-BETRG,
        SEPAR_REP_CUR   LIKE RT-BETRG,
        HONOR_REP_CUR   LIKE RT-BETRG,
        SEPAR_REP_PRE1  LIKE RT-BETRG,
        HONOR_REP_PRE1  LIKE RT-BETRG,
        SEPAR_REP_PRE2  LIKE RT-BETRG,
        HONOR_REP_PRE2  LIKE RT-BETRG,
        SEPRP_DEP_CUR   LIKE RT-BETRG                       "N1751076
        .
*-------------------------------------------------------------------
* Sepration payment(Current work place)
  PERFORM READ_RT_BETRG USING PKR99_WTR_SEPARATION CHANGING AMOUNT.
  SEPAR_CUR = AMOUNT.
* Add insurance pay to legal separation pay
  PERFORM READ_RT_BETRG USING PKR99_WTR_INSURANCE CHANGING AMOUNT.
  SEPAR_CUR = SEPAR_CUR + AMOUNT.
* Addtional sepration payment(Current work place)
  PERFORM READ_RT_BETRG USING PKR99_WTR_ADDITION   CHANGING AMOUNT.

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  VALIDITY_CHECK
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       [Log]  Create: N1536031
*       [Log]  Modify: N1561401
*                      N1559867: Tax Deferring DME
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM VALIDITY_CHECK USING P_EXIT.
  DATA: P_VALDT TYPE D,
        P_OCCAT LIKE RGDIR-OCCAT,
        P_SEQNR LIKE RGDIR-SEQNR.
  DATA: P_RGDIR LIKE RGDIR OCCURS 0 WITH HEADER LINE.

  CLEAR: OSPDT. OCCAT = RGDIR-OCCAT. P_EXIT = 0.
  P_OCCAT = RGDIR-OCCAT.  P_SEQNR = RGDIR-SEQNR.  P_VALDT = RGDIR-PAYDT.

* Set value for global variants 'occat' and 'ospdt'
  IF P_OCCAT = PKR99_OCC_SEP_TAX_DEF.
    READ TABLE P0538 WITH KEY BEGDA = RGDIR-PAYDT ADJST = '1'.
    OSPDT = P_VALDT = P0538-OSPDT.
    "Get original off-cycle category
    IF OSPDT BETWEEN START_DT AND END_DT.
      P_RGDIR[] = RGDIR[].
    ELSE.
      PERFORM READ_RGDIR_WITH_DATE TABLES P_RGDIR
                                   USING OSPDT.
    ENDIF.
    LOOP AT P_RGDIR WHERE PAYDT = OSPDT AND
           ( OCCAT = PKR99_OCC_SEP OR OCCAT = PKR99_OCC_MID_SEP ).
      OCCAT = P_RGDIR-OCCAT.
      EXIT.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDIF.

*<<< N1559867 Continue only when off-cycle 0012 for tax deferring
  IF TDF_FORM = 'X' AND P_OCCAT <> PKR99_OCC_SEP_TAX_DEF.
    P_EXIT = 1.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.
*>>> N1559867

* DME file output check
  IF OBJECTR = 'X'.
    IF TDF_FORM <> 'X' AND  P_OCCAT = PKR99_OCC_SEP_TAX_DEF.
*     Check if off-cycle 0009 or 0011 payroll since original payroll
      LOOP AT P_RGDIR WHERE PAYDT > OSPDT AND PAYDT < RGDIR-PAYDT AND
              ( OCCAT = PKR99_OCC_SEP OR OCCAT = PKR99_OCC_MID_SEP ).
        P_EXIT = 1.
        EXIT.
      ENDLOOP.
    ENDIF.

    CHECK: P_EXIT = 0.
*   OC 0009: separation date
*   OC 0011: agreed payment date->original pay date->actual pay date
    LOOP AT P0538 WHERE BEGDA EQ P_VALDT.
      IF OCCAT = PKR99_OCC_SEP.
        P_VALDT = P0538-SEPDT.
      ELSEIF OCCAT = PKR99_OCC_MID_SEP.
        IF NOT P0538-AGPDT IS INITIAL.
          P_VALDT = P0538-AGPDT.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
    IF P_VALDT GE START_DT AND P_VALDT LE END_DT.
      P_EXIT = 0.
    ELSE.
      P_EXIT = 1.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  "Start of HUANGHA1448559
  "Exclude current year run period for next year separation
*  LOOP AT p0538 WHERE begda EQ p_begda.
*    IF ( p0538-agpdt GE start_dt AND p0538-agpdt LE end_dt ) OR
*       ( p0538-sepdt GE start_dt AND p0538-sepdt LE end_dt AND
*         P0538-agpdt IS INITIAL ).                      "N1379685
*      IF NOT p0538-agpdt IS INITIAL.
*        start_dt = p0538-agpdt.
*        end_dt = p0538-agpdt.
*      ELSEIF NOT p0538-sepdt IS INITIAL.
*        start_dt = p0538-sepdt.
*        end_dt = p0538-sepdt.
*      ENDIF.
*      p_exit = 0.
*      EXIT.
*    ELSE.
*      p_exit = 1.
*      EXIT.
*    ENDIF.
*  ENDLOOP.
  "End of HUANGHA1448559
*>>> N1561401
ENDFORM. " VALIDITY_CHECK

FORM FILL_DME .
  IF START_DT(4) GE '2010'.
    IF OBJECTR = 'X'.
      PERFORM MAKE_INCOME_LIST1.
      PERFORM MAKE_INCOME_LIST2.
      PERFORM FILL_ITAB_DME_HON.
    ENDIF.
  ELSE.
    IF OBJECTR = 'X' AND LSP_FORM = 'X'.                    "N1559867
      IF EE_TYPE = 'LSP'.
        PERFORM MAKE_INCOME_LIST1.
        PERFORM MAKE_INCOME_LIST2.
        PERFORM FILL_ITAB_DME.
      ENDIF.
*    ELSEIF OBJECTH  = 'X'.                             "N1559867
    ELSEIF OBJECTR = 'X' AND HON_FORM = 'X'.                "N1559867
      IF EE_TYPE  = 'HON'.
        PERFORM MAKE_INCOME_LIST1.
        PERFORM MAKE_INCOME_LIST2.
        PERFORM FILL_ITAB_DME_HON.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

* QU796286
*    IF objectr = 'X'.
*      PERFORM make_income_list1.
*      PERFORM make_income_list2.
*      PERFORM fill_itab_dme.
ENDFORM. " FILL_DME

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       [Log]  Create: N1536031
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_2010 USING FILE_TYPE. "N1559867
  PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_10 USING 'A' FILE_TYPE.     "N1559867

  SORT B_RECORD     BY BUKRS SEQNO.
  SORT C_RECORD_HON BY BUKRS TAX_OFF STCD2 SEQNO.
  SORT D_RECORD     BY BUKRS TAX_OFF SEQNO D_SEQNO.

  LOOP AT B_RECORD WHERE BUKRS = A_RECORD-BUKRS.
    PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_10 USING 'B' FILE_TYPE.   "N1559867
    LOOP AT C_RECORD_HON WHERE TAX_OFF = B_RECORD-TAX_OFF
                           AND STCD2   = B_RECORD-STCD2
                           AND BUKRS   = B_RECORD-BUKRS.
      PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_10 USING 'C' FILE_TYPE. "N1559867
      LOOP AT D_RECORD  WHERE PERNR = C_RECORD_HON-PERNR.
        PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_10 USING 'D' FILE_TYPE. "N1559867
      ENDLOOP.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDLOOP.
ENDFORM. " WRITE_TO_TEMSE_2010

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_10
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       [Log]  Create: N1536031
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->RECORDNAME   NAME OF RECORD (A/B/C/D)
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_10 USING RECORDNAME
                                      FILE_TYPE.            "N1559867
  IF FILE_TYPE = 'LSP'.
    CLEAR DATAFILE_10. REFRESH DATAFILE_10.

    CASE RECORDNAME.
      WHEN 'A'.
        PERFORM  ADD_ARECORD_TO_DATAFILE_10.
      WHEN 'B'.
        PERFORM  ADD_BRECORD_TO_DATAFILE_10.
      WHEN 'C'.
        PERFORM  ADD_CRECORD_TO_DATAFILE_10.
      WHEN 'D'.
        PERFORM  ADD_DRECORD_TO_DATAFILE_10.
    ENDCASE.
    CALL FUNCTION 'RSTS_WRITE'
      EXPORTING
        FBHANDLE = OBJHD
      TABLES
        DATATAB  = DATAFILE_10.
  ELSEIF FILE_TYPE = 'TDF'.
    CLEAR DATAFILE_TDF. REFRESH DATAFILE_TDF.

    CASE RECORDNAME.
      WHEN 'A'.
        PERFORM  ADD_ARECORD_TO_DATAFILE_TDF.
      WHEN 'B'.
        PERFORM  ADD_BRECORD_TO_DATAFILE_TDF.
      WHEN 'C'.
        PERFORM  ADD_CRECORD_TO_DATAFILE_TDF.
      WHEN 'D'.
        PERFORM  ADD_DRECORD_TO_DATAFILE_TDF.
    ENDCASE.
    CALL FUNCTION 'RSTS_WRITE'
      EXPORTING
        FBHANDLE = OBJHD
      TABLES
        DATATAB  = DATAFILE_TDF.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM. " WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_10

FORM ADD_ARECORD_TO_DATAFILE_10 .
  MOVE: A_RECORD-R_MODIF        TO DATAFILE_10+0(1),
        A_RECORD-D_MODIF_HON    TO DATAFILE_10+1(2),
        A_RECORD-TAX_OFF        TO DATAFILE_10+3(3),
        A_RECORD-SUBMITDT       TO DATAFILE_10+6(8),
        A_RECORD-AGENCY         TO DATAFILE_10+14(1),
        A_RECORD-AGENT_NUM      TO DATAFILE_10+15(6),
        A_RECORD-HOME_TAX_ID    TO DATAFILE_10+21(20),
        A_RECORD-ITEM8_SPACE    TO DATAFILE_10+41(4),
        A_RECORD-STCD2          TO DATAFILE_10+45(10),
        A_RECORD-COMNM          TO DATAFILE_10+55(40),
        A_RECORD-SUB_DEPT       TO DATAFILE_10+95(30),
        A_RECORD-SUB_NAME       TO DATAFILE_10+125(30),
        A_RECORD-AG_TELNR       TO DATAFILE_10+155(15),
        A_RECORD-B_REC_NUM      TO DATAFILE_10+170(5),
        A_RECORD-HANGLE_CODE    TO DATAFILE_10+175(3),
        SPACE                   TO DATAFILE_10+178(802).

* Unicode
  IF CL_ABAP_CHAR_UTILITIES=>CHARSIZE > 1.
    CLEAR  OUT_XSTRING.  CLEAR  ADJ_STRING. CLEAR  ADJ_XSTRING.
* Convert unicode string to hex stream
    PERFORM UNISTRING_TO_XSTRING USING   DATAFILE_10+0(41)  41.
    MOVE OUT_XSTRING TO  ADJ_XSTRING.

    PERFORM UNISTRING_TO_XSTRING USING   DATAFILE_10+41(54) 54.
    CONCATENATE ADJ_XSTRING OUT_XSTRING INTO ADJ_XSTRING IN BYTE MODE.

    PERFORM UNISTRING_TO_XSTRING USING   DATAFILE_10+95(30) 30.
    CONCATENATE ADJ_XSTRING OUT_XSTRING INTO ADJ_XSTRING IN BYTE MODE.

    PERFORM UNISTRING_TO_XSTRING USING   DATAFILE_10+125(30) 30.
    CONCATENATE ADJ_XSTRING OUT_XSTRING INTO ADJ_XSTRING IN BYTE MODE.

    PERFORM UNISTRING_TO_XSTRING USING   DATAFILE_10+155(825) 825.
    CONCATENATE ADJ_XSTRING OUT_XSTRING INTO ADJ_XSTRING IN BYTE MODE.
* Comvert hex stream to unicode string
    PERFORM  XSTRING_TO_UNISTRING.
    CLEAR DATAFILE_10. REFRESH DATAFILE_10.
    APPEND ADJ_STRING TO DATAFILE_10.
* Unicode end
  ELSE.
    APPEND DATAFILE_10.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM. " ADD_ARECORD_TO_DATAFILE_10

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  READ_RGDIR_WITH_DATE
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       [Log]  Create: N1561401
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM READ_RGDIR_WITH_DATE TABLES P_RGDIR
                          USING P_DATE.

  DATA: TEM_RGDIR LIKE RGDIR OCCURS 0.

  CALL FUNCTION 'CU_READ_RGDIR'
    EXPORTING
      PERSNR          = PERNR-PERNR
    TABLES
      IN_RGDIR        = TEM_RGDIR
    EXCEPTIONS
      NO_RECORD_FOUND = 1
      OTHERS          = 2.

  IF SY-SUBRC NE 0.
    PERFORM ERROR_HANDLING USING PERNR-PERNR '405'
                                 SPACE SPACE SPACE SPACE.

    REJECT.
  ENDIF.

  CALL FUNCTION 'CD_READ_DATE_RANGE_ACTUAL_NV'
    EXPORTING
      BEGDA     = P_DATE
      ENDDA     = P_DATE
    TABLES
      RGDIR_IN  = TEM_RGDIR
      RGDIR_OUT = P_RGDIR.
ENDFORM. "READ_RGDIR_WITH_DATE

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  MODIFY_SCREEN
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       [Log]  Create: N1559867
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM MODIFY_SCREEN .
*<<<N1473213 - add MODIF ID for hiding Honorable form and DME
  DATA: IND TYPE C VALUE 0.

  LOOP AT SCREEN.
    IF SCREEN-GROUP1 = 'HON' AND END_DT(4) >= 2010.
      SCREEN-INVISIBLE = '1'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
      "in case of variant, clear form name for HON format.
      HON_FORM = ' '.
*    ELSEIF "( end_dt(4) < 2010 OR lsp_form <> 'X' ) AND
*       screen-group1 = 'COP'.
*      IF end_dt(4) < 2011.
*        IF end_dt(4) < 2010 OR ( lsp_form <> 'X').
*          screen-invisible = '1'.
*          p_kbee = 'X'. p_kber = p_rber = ' '.
*        ENDIF.
*        IF screen-name = 'P_RBIR'.
*          screen-invisible = '1'.
*        ENDIF.
*        MODIFY SCREEN.
*      ELSE.
*        IF  lsp_form <> 'X' AND tdf_form <> 'X'.
*          screen-invisible = '1'.
*          p_kbee = 'X'. p_kber = p_rber = ' '.
*        ELSEIF lsp_form = 'X' AND screen-name = 'P_RBIR'.
*          screen-invisible = '1'.
*        ENDIF.
*        MODIFY SCREEN.
*      ENDIF.
    ELSEIF SCREEN-GROUP1 = 'COP'.
      IF END_DT(4) >= 2011 .
        IF LSP_FORM <> 'X' AND TDF_FORM <> 'X' .
          IND = 1.
        ENDIF.
      ELSEIF END_DT(4) < 2010 OR LSP_FORM <> 'X' .
        IND = 1.
      ENDIF.
      IF IND = 1.
        SCREEN-INVISIBLE = '1'.
        MODIFY SCREEN.
        P_KBEE = 'X'. P_KBER = P_RBER = ' '.
        P_KBEE = 'X'. P_KBER = P_RBER = ' '.
      ENDIF.
    ELSEIF SCREEN-GROUP1 = 'ESP' AND END_DT(4) < 2010.
      SCREEN-INVISIBLE = '1'.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
      ESP_FORM = ' '.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.
*>>>N1473213
  IF END_DT(4) >= 2013.                                     "N1800801
    CLEAR TDF_FORM.                                         "N1800801
  ENDIF.
*<<< N1559867 Disable Data Medium Creation
  LOOP AT SCREEN.
    IF SCREEN-GROUP1 = 'DME' OR SCREEN-GROUP1 = 'SBP' OR
*===========2013.01.31
        SCREEN-GROUP1 = 'ALV' OR
*=======================================
      SCREEN-GROUP1 = 'SUB'.
      IF ESP_FORM = 'X'.
        OBJECT = 'X'.
        OBJECTR = ' '.
        SCREEN-INPUT = '0'.
      ELSE.
        SCREEN-INPUT = '1'.
      ENDIF.
*>>>N1800801- hide Tax deferring DME from 2012
      IF TDF_FORM = 'X' AND END_DT(4) >= 2012 AND
        ( SCREEN-GROUP1 = 'DME' OR SCREEN-GROUP1 = 'SUB' OR
*===========2013.01.31
           SCREEN-GROUP1 = 'ALV' OR
*=======================================
        SCREEN-GROUP1 = 'SBP' ).
        SCREEN-INPUT = '0'.
      ENDIF.
*<<<N1800801
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.
*>>> N1559867
*>>>N1713857 hide tax deferring form from 2013.
  LOOP AT SCREEN.
    IF SCREEN-GROUP1 = 'TDF' AND END_DT(4) > 2012.
      SCREEN-INVISIBLE = '1'.

      TDF_FORM = ' '.
    ENDIF.
