*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  ADD_ARECORD_TO_DATAFILE_TDF
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       [Log]  Create: N1559867
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM ADD_ARECORD_TO_DATAFILE_TDF.
  MOVE:   A_RECORD-R_MODIF      TO DATAFILE_TDF+0(1),
          A_RECORD-TAX_OFF      TO DATAFILE_TDF+1(3),
          A_RECORD-SUBMITDT     TO DATAFILE_TDF+4(8),
          A_RECORD-STCD2        TO DATAFILE_TDF+12(10),
          A_RECORD-COMNM        TO DATAFILE_TDF+22(40),
          A_RECORD-SUB_DEPT     TO DATAFILE_TDF+62(30),
          A_RECORD-SUB_NAME     TO DATAFILE_TDF+92(30),
          A_RECORD-AG_TELNR     TO DATAFILE_TDF+122(15).
  UNPACK: A_RECORD-B_REC_NUM    TO DATAFILE_TDF+137(10),
          A_RECORD-C_REC_NUM    TO DATAFILE_TDF+147(10),
          A_RECORD-D_REC_NUM    TO DATAFILE_TDF+157(10).
  MOVE:   SPACE                 TO DATAFILE_TDF+167(256).

* Unicode
  IF CL_ABAP_CHAR_UTILITIES=>CHARSIZE > 1.
    CLEAR  OUT_XSTRING.  CLEAR  ADJ_STRING. CLEAR  ADJ_XSTRING.
* Convert unicode string to hex stream
    PERFORM UNISTRING_TO_XSTRING USING   DATAFILE_TDF+0(62)  62.
    MOVE OUT_XSTRING TO  ADJ_XSTRING.

    PERFORM UNISTRING_TO_XSTRING USING   DATAFILE_TDF+62(30) 30.
    CONCATENATE ADJ_XSTRING OUT_XSTRING INTO ADJ_XSTRING IN BYTE MODE.

    PERFORM UNISTRING_TO_XSTRING USING   DATAFILE_TDF+92(30) 30.
    CONCATENATE ADJ_XSTRING OUT_XSTRING INTO ADJ_XSTRING IN BYTE MODE.

    PERFORM UNISTRING_TO_XSTRING USING   DATAFILE_TDF+122(301) 301.
    CONCATENATE ADJ_XSTRING OUT_XSTRING INTO ADJ_XSTRING IN BYTE MODE.
* Comvert hex stream to unicode string
    PERFORM  XSTRING_TO_UNISTRING.
    CLEAR DATAFILE_TDF. REFRESH DATAFILE_TDF.
    APPEND ADJ_STRING TO DATAFILE_TDF.
* Unicode end
  ELSE.
    APPEND DATAFILE_TDF.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM. " ADD_ARECORD_TO_DATAFILE_TDF

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  WRITE_TO_TEMSE_2011
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_FILE_TYPE  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_2011 USING FILE_TYPE.
  PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_11 USING 'A' FILE_TYPE.

  SORT B_RECORD     BY BUKRS SEQNO.
  SORT C_RECORD_HON BY BUKRS TAX_OFF STCD2 SEQNO.
  SORT D_RECORD     BY BUKRS TAX_OFF SEQNO D_SEQNO.

  LOOP AT B_RECORD WHERE BUKRS = A_RECORD-BUKRS.
    PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_11 USING 'B' FILE_TYPE.
    LOOP AT C_RECORD_HON WHERE TAX_OFF = B_RECORD-TAX_OFF
                           AND STCD2   = B_RECORD-STCD2
                           AND BUKRS   = B_RECORD-BUKRS.
      PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_11 USING 'C' FILE_TYPE.
      LOOP AT D_RECORD  WHERE PERNR = C_RECORD_HON-PERNR.
        PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_11 USING 'D' FILE_TYPE.
      ENDLOOP.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDLOOP.
ENDFORM. " WRITE_TO_TEMSE_2011
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_11
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_8915   text
*      -->P_FILE_TYPE  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_11 USING RECORDNAME
                                        FILE_TYPE.
  IF FILE_TYPE = 'LSP'.
    CLEAR DATAFILE_11. REFRESH DATAFILE_11.

    CASE RECORDNAME.
      WHEN 'A'.
        PERFORM  ADD_ARECORD_TO_DATAFILE_11.
      WHEN 'B'.
        PERFORM  ADD_BRECORD_TO_DATAFILE_11.
      WHEN 'C'.
        PERFORM  ADD_CRECORD_TO_DATAFILE_11.
      WHEN 'D'.
        PERFORM  ADD_DRECORD_TO_DATAFILE_11.
    ENDCASE.
    CALL FUNCTION 'RSTS_WRITE'
      EXPORTING
        FBHANDLE = OBJHD
      TABLES
        DATATAB  = DATAFILE_11.
  ELSEIF FILE_TYPE = 'TDF'.
    CLEAR DATAFILE_TDF_11. REFRESH DATAFILE_TDF_11.

    CASE RECORDNAME.
      WHEN 'A'.
        PERFORM  ADD_ARECORD_TO_DATAFILE_TDF_11.
      WHEN 'B'.
        PERFORM  ADD_BRECORD_TO_DATAFILE_TDF_11.
      WHEN 'C'.
        PERFORM  ADD_CRECORD_TO_DATAFILE_TDF_11.
      WHEN 'D'.
        PERFORM  ADD_DRECORD_TO_DATAFILE_TDF_11.
    ENDCASE.
    CALL FUNCTION 'RSTS_WRITE'
      EXPORTING
        FBHANDLE = OBJHD
      TABLES
        DATATAB  = DATAFILE_TDF_11.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM. " WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_11
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  ADD_ARECORD_TO_DATAFILE_11
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM ADD_ARECORD_TO_DATAFILE_11 .
  MOVE: A_RECORD-R_MODIF        TO DATAFILE_11+0(1),

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  CHECK_SAME_YEAR
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       [Log]  Create: N1688394
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM CHECK_SAME_YEAR USING P_PAYDT TYPE D
                            P_KRSP1 LIKE KRSP1
                      CHANGING P_SAME_YEAR.
  DATA: P_CHKDT       TYPE D,
        P_TAX_YEAR(4) TYPE C.

  READ TABLE P0538 WITH KEY BEGDA = P_PAYDT ADJST = '1'.
  IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
    P_CHKDT = P0538-OSPDT.
  ELSE.
    P_CHKDT = P_PAYDT.
  ENDIF.

  IF OCCAT = PKR99_OCC_MID_SEP.
    IF NOT P_KRSP1-AGPDT IS INITIAL.
      P_TAX_YEAR = P_KRSP1-AGPDT(4).
    ELSE.
      P_TAX_YEAR = P_PAYDT(4).
    ENDIF.
  ELSE.
    P_TAX_YEAR = P_KRSP1-SEPDT(4).
  ENDIF.

  SORT P0538 BY ENDDA DESCENDING.
  LOOP AT P0538 WHERE BEGDA < P_CHKDT AND ADJST = ' '.
*   The last one is mid-separation, check pay date or agreed pay date
    IF NOT P0538-AGPDT IS INITIAL AND P0538-AGPDT(4) = P_TAX_YEAR.
      P_SAME_YEAR = 'X'.
    ELSEIF P0538-BEGDA(4) = P_TAX_YEAR.
      P_SAME_YEAR = 'X'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.
ENDFORM. " CHECK_SAME_YEAR
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  WRITE_TO_TEMSE_2012
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       N1800801 [CREATE] - 2012 separation DME
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->FILE_TYPE  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_2012 USING FILE_TYPE.
  PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_12 USING 'A' FILE_TYPE.

  SORT B_RECORD     BY BUKRS SEQNO.
  SORT C_RECORD_HON BY BUKRS TAX_OFF STCD2 SEQNO.
  SORT D_RECORD     BY BUKRS TAX_OFF SEQNO D_SEQNO.

  LOOP AT B_RECORD WHERE BUKRS = A_RECORD-BUKRS.
    PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_12 USING 'B' FILE_TYPE.
    LOOP AT C_RECORD_HON WHERE TAX_OFF = B_RECORD-TAX_OFF
                           AND STCD2   = B_RECORD-STCD2
                           AND BUKRS   = B_RECORD-BUKRS.
      PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_12 USING 'C' FILE_TYPE.
      LOOP AT D_RECORD  WHERE PERNR = C_RECORD_HON-PERNR.
        PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_12 USING 'D' FILE_TYPE.
      ENDLOOP.
      LOOP AT E_RECORD  WHERE PERNR = C_RECORD_HON-PERNR.   "N1800801
        PERFORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_12 USING 'E' FILE_TYPE.
      ENDLOOP.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDLOOP.
ENDFORM. " WRITE_TO_TEMSE_2012
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_12
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       N1800801-[CREATE]
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->recordname   text
*      -->file_type  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_12 USING RECORDNAME
                                        FILE_TYPE.
  IF FILE_TYPE = 'LSP'.
    CLEAR DATAFILE_12. REFRESH DATAFILE_12.

    CASE RECORDNAME.
      WHEN 'A'.
        PERFORM  ADD_ARECORD_TO_DATAFILE_12.
      WHEN 'B'.
        PERFORM  ADD_BRECORD_TO_DATAFILE_12.
      WHEN 'C'.
        PERFORM  ADD_CRECORD_TO_DATAFILE_12.
      WHEN 'D'.
        PERFORM  ADD_DRECORD_TO_DATAFILE_12.
      WHEN 'E'.
        PERFORM  ADD_ERECORD_TO_DATAFILE_12.
    ENDCASE.
    CALL FUNCTION 'RSTS_WRITE'
      EXPORTING
        FBHANDLE = OBJHD
      TABLES
        DATATAB  = DATAFILE_12.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM. " WRITE_TO_TEMSE_RECORD_12

          B_RECORD-STCD1           TO  DATAFILE_12+92(13),
          B_RECORD-TAXPAYER        TO  DATAFILE_12+105(1).
  UNPACK: B_RECORD-C_REC_NUM       TO  DATAFILE_12+106(7),
          B_RECORD-D_REC_NUM       TO  DATAFILE_12+113(7),
          B_RECORD-TOT_SEPAR       TO  DATAFILE_12+120(14),
          B_RECORD-INC_TAX         TO  DATAFILE_12+134(13),
          B_RECORD-LEGAL_TAX       TO  DATAFILE_12+147(13),
          B_RECORD-DET_RTAX        TO  DATAFILE_12+160(13),
          B_RECORD-DET_STAX        TO  DATAFILE_12+173(13),
          B_RECORD-DET_TOTAL       TO  DATAFILE_12+186(13),
          B_RECORD-SUBMIT_PERIOD   TO  DATAFILE_12+199(1).
  MOVE:   SPACE                    TO  DATAFILE_12+200(810).

* Unicode
  IF CL_ABAP_CHAR_UTILITIES=>CHARSIZE > 1.
    CLEAR  OUT_XSTRING.  CLEAR  ADJ_STRING. CLEAR  ADJ_XSTRING.
* Convert unicode string to hex stream
    PERFORM UNISTRING_TO_XSTRING USING   DATAFILE_12+0(62)  62.
    MOVE OUT_XSTRING TO  ADJ_XSTRING.

    PERFORM UNISTRING_TO_XSTRING USING   DATAFILE_12+62(30) 30.
    CONCATENATE ADJ_XSTRING OUT_XSTRING INTO ADJ_XSTRING IN BYTE MODE.

    PERFORM UNISTRING_TO_XSTRING USING   DATAFILE_12+92(918) 918.
    CONCATENATE ADJ_XSTRING OUT_XSTRING INTO ADJ_XSTRING IN BYTE MODE.
* Comvert hex stream to unicode string
    PERFORM  XSTRING_TO_UNISTRING.

    APPEND ADJ_STRING TO DATAFILE_12.
* Unicode end
  ELSE.
    APPEND DATAFILE_12.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM. " ADD_BRECORD_TO_DATAFILE_12

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  FILL_E_RECORD
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       N1800801-[CREATE]
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM FILL_E_RECORD .
  CLEAR E_RECORD.
  CHECK ITAB_C-ADJST = 'X'.                                 "N1814910
  MOVE PERNR-PERNR            TO E_RECORD-PERNR.
  MOVE C_RECORD_HON-BUKRS     TO E_RECORD-BUKRS.
  MOVE : 'E'                  TO E_RECORD-R_MODIF,
         '25'                 TO E_RECORD-D_MODIF,
         C_RECORD_HON-TAX_OFF TO E_RECORD-TAX_OFF,
         C_RECORD_HON-SEQNO   TO E_RECORD-SEQNO,
         C_RECORD_HON-STCD2   TO E_RECORD-STCD2,
         SPACE                TO E_RECORD-ESPACE,
         C_RECORD_HON-REGNO   TO E_RECORD-REGNO.

  MOVE-CORRESPONDING ITAB_C   TO E_RECORD.
  MOVE 1                      TO E_RECORD-E_SEQNO.
  APPEND E_RECORD.
ENDFORM. " FILL_E_RECORD
*<<< N1831602 Terry Begin
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  FILL_SP_2013
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM FILL_SP_2013 .
  DATA: AMOUNT          LIKE RT-BETRG,
        TOTAL           LIKE RT-BETRG,
        TMP_TTL         LIKE RT-BETRG,
        SEPAR_CUR       LIKE RT-BETRG,
        SEPAR_PRE1      LIKE RT-BETRG,
        SEPAR_DEP_CUR   LIKE RT-BETRG,
        L_TAXBLE_BASE   LIKE RT-BETRG,
        L_TOT_BAS_DED   LIKE RT-BETRG,
        L_SEP_PED_DED   LIKE RT-BETRG.
*-------------------------------------------------------------------
* Sepration payment(Current work place)
  PERFORM READ_RT_BETRG USING PKR99_WTR_TOT_SEPARATION CHANGING AMOUNT.
  SEPAR_CUR = AMOUNT.
  PERFORM READ_RT_BETRG USING PKR99_WTR_TOT_SEPARATION_HON
                        CHANGING AMOUNT.
  SEPAR_CUR = SEPAR_CUR + AMOUNT.
* Deposit legal part (Current work place)
  PERFORM READ_RT_BETRG USING PKR99_WT_DEPOSIT_LSP CHANGING AMOUNT.
  SEPAR_DEP_CUR = SEPAR_DEP_CUR + AMOUNT.
  PERFORM READ_RT_BETRG USING PKR99_WT_DEPOSIT_HON CHANGING AMOUNT.
  SEPAR_DEP_CUR = SEPAR_DEP_CUR + AMOUNT.
  PERFORM READ_RT_BETRG USING PKR99_WT_DEPOSIT_LSP_PRE CHANGING AMOUNT.
  SEPAR_DEP_CUR = SEPAR_DEP_CUR + AMOUNT.
  PERFORM READ_RT_BETRG USING PKR99_WT_DEPOSIT_HON_PRE CHANGING AMOUNT.
  SEPAR_DEP_CUR = SEPAR_DEP_CUR + AMOUNT.
  PERFORM READ_RT_BETRG USING PKR99_WT_DEPOSIT_REP CHANGING AMOUNT.
  SEPAR_DEP_CUR = SEPAR_DEP_CUR + AMOUNT.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------
* Payment infomation from 1st ex-company
  MOVE  KRSP1-BUS01  TO ITAB_C-BUS01.
  PERFORM RE7KRP1 USING ITAB_C-BUS01.
  MOVE T7KRP1-COMNM  TO ITAB_C-PRE_COMNM.
* Separation payment from 1st previous company
  SEPAR_PRE1 = KRSP1-SPY01 + KRSP1-IPY01 + KRSP1-SPY02 + KRSP1-IPY02
             + KRSP1-HPY01 + KRSP1-HPY02.
  SEPAR_CUR = SEPAR_CUR - SEPAR_PRE1.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------
* Set Adjust Indicator for printing tax convert session
  IF SEPAR_DEP_CUR > 0.
    ITAB_C-ADJST = 'X'.
  ENDIF.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------
  PUT_WAGE_TYPE   PKR99_WTR_SER_PED_DED    ITAB_C-SEP_PED_DED.
  PUT_WAGE_TYPE   PKR99_WTR_TOT_BAS_DED    ITAB_C-TOT_BAS_DED.
* Sum to totals and put to itab_c.
  CHANGE_CURR: SEPAR_CUR      ITAB_C-SPY00,
               SEPAR_PRE1     ITAB_C-SPY01,
               SEPAR_DEP_CUR  ITAB_C-DEPCU_SEP.

  TMP_TTL = SEPAR_CUR + SEPAR_PRE1.
  TOTAL   = TOTAL + TMP_TTL.
  CHANGE_CURR  TMP_TTL        ITAB_C-SPTOT.

  PERFORM READ_RT_BETRG USING PKR99_WTR_SER_PED_DED
                        CHANGING L_SEP_PED_DED.
  PERFORM READ_RT_BETRG USING PKR99_WTR_TOT_BAS_DED
                        CHANGING L_TOT_BAS_DED.
  L_TAXBLE_BASE = TOTAL - L_TOT_BAS_DED - L_SEP_PED_DED.
  CHANGE_CURR  L_TAXBLE_BASE ITAB_C-TAXBLE_BASE.

  TMP_TTL = SEPAR_DEP_CUR.
  CHANGE_CURR  TMP_TTL        ITAB_C-DEPTT.

  PUT_WAGE_TYPE   PKR99_WTR_SER_PED_DED    ITAB_C-SEP_PED_DED.
  PUT_WAGE_TYPE   PKR99_WTR_TOT_BAS_DED    ITAB_C-TOT_BAS_DED.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------
* Fill in IRA deposit information
  READ TABLE P0538 WITH KEY BEGDA = RGDIR-BONDT ADJST = '1'.
*  IF sy-subrc = 0 AND tdf_form = 'X'.                      "N1713857
  IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
    ITAB_C-IRA_NAME     = P0538-IRANM.
    ITAB_C-IRA_BIZ_NO   = P0538-IRABN.
    ITAB_C-DEPOSIT_ACC  = P0538-DEPAC.
*<<<N1559867: remove possible '-' in account.
    REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF '-' IN ITAB_C-IRA_BIZ_NO WITH ''.
    REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF '-' IN ITAB_C-DEPOSIT_ACC WITH ''.
*>>>N1559867
    ITAB_C-DEPOSIT_DATE = P0538-DEPDT.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM. " FILL_SP_2013
*>>> N1831602 Terry End
